avarai vakaikaḷiṟ cila uruṇṭu tiraṇṭiruppiṉum, perumpāṉmai nōkki avai vilakkākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum, piñcu nilaiyilēyē camaikkumāṟu maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatāl, ellā vakaikaḷum piñcu nilaiyil cappaiyākavē yiruttalai nōkkuka.
ovvoru poruḷum ataṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟiyē peyar peṟuvatu marapu. vāḻai, kattari, muruṅkai, veṇṭai, pūcaṇi, curai, pīrkku, puṭalai, pākal mutaliya piṟa kāyvakaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟōṭum oppunōkki, avarai yoṉṟē taṭṭaiyā yiruttalaik kaṇṭaṟika.
paḷḷam eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ mēlmaṭṭattiṉ tāḻvu; kuṭṭai eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ uyarattiṉ tāḻvu; cappai eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ tiṇṇattiṉ allatu puṭaippiṉ tāḻvu. im muk karuttum ōriṉappaṭṭaṉa. āḻamillāta kiṇṇam taṭṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
poruḷvakai
(1)	āṭṭuk kompavarai = āṭṭuk kompupōl vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that resembles goat’s horn in shape.
(2)	ārāl mīṉavarai = ārāl mīṉ pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that resembles sand - eel in shape.
(3)	āṉaik kātavarai = āṉaik kātu pōl vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that resembles elephant’s ear in shape.
(4)	kaṇuvavarai = koṭiyiṉ kaṇukkaḷil kāykkum avarai; kind of bean that brings forth fruits at joints also.
(5)	koḻuppavarai = kuṭṭaiyākak koḻutta cataiyuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that is short and fleshy.
(6)	kōḻiyavarai (kōḻikkā lavarai) = kōḻi viral pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; canavalia gladiata.
(7)	civappavarai - cevvavarai = cenniṟa avarai; a red variety of bean, lablab cultratus.
(8)	ciṟṟavarai - maṇiyavarai = a small variety of bean.
(9)	tīvāntara vavarai = kīḻait tīviṉiṉṟu vanta avarai;  a bean from the eastern islands (w.).
(10)	nakaravarai - dolichos rugosus (W)
(11)	pālavarai - veḷḷavarai; a white variety of bean, 
	avaraik koḻuṅkoṭi viḷarkkāy
	kōṭpata māka.  (puṟam 120. 10-11)
(12)	pēravarai - a large variety of bean.
(13)	muṟukkavarai = tirukal muṟukalākavuḷḷa avarai; psopha carpus tetragonolobus.
iṉapporuḷkaḷ
(1)	kappal avarai = french beans.
(2)	kāṭṭavarai = koḻuppavarai pōṉṟatāyuḷḷa moccai (cēlam vaḻakku); wild bean, lablab vulgaris.
vīṭṭup puṟattil viḷaivikkappaṭum avaraiyai nōkki moccai kāṭṭavarai eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, avaraikku vīṭṭavarai eṉa ōr aṭaimoḻip peyar peṟappaṭum.
vīṭṭavaraikkum kāṭṭavaraikkum vēṟupāṭu
		vīṭṭavarai		kāṭṭavarai
(1)	vīṭṭup puṟattil 		kāṭṭup puṟattil
	viḷaikkappaṭuvatu 	viḷaikkappaṭuvatu
(2)	koṭiyākap paṭarvatu	kuttuc ceṭiyāka vaḷarvatu
(3)	iḷaṅkāy kaṟiyākac 	muṟṟiya kāy avittuc
	camaikkappaṭuvatu		 tiṉṉappaṭuvatu
(4)	tōl melliyatu	tōl valliyatu.
(3) ayalnāṭṭu (cīmai) avarai = marutaṅkāy pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷatum nīṇṭatumāṉa veḷināṭṭuk kāyvakai; a kind of foreign vegetable. ayal nāṭṭai allatu mēl nāṭṭaip potu makkaḷ cīmai eṉṉum vaṭacollāṟ kuṟippar. iṅku vaṭacol eṉṟatu vaṭanāṭṭuc collai.
(4) cīṉiyavarai - kottavarai - kuttavarai = kottuk kottākak kāykkum kāyvakai; a species of pulse whose fruits are in bunches.
(5) cuṭalaiyavarai = cuṭukāṭṭup pakkattil iyaṟkaiyāka viḷaivatum, cīṉiyavarai pōṉṟatum, uṇṇap paṭātatumāṉa kāyvakai; a species of inedible bean growing wildly near burning grounds. itu pēyavaraiyeṉavumpaṭum.
(6) pāṭavarai - vāḷavarai - tampaṭṭavarai - cāṭṭavarai = vāḷ pōl nīṇṭu paṭṭaiyāka virukkum kāyvakai; sword-bean, canavalia ensiformis. 
iṉaccol
avarai	te(luṅku)	-	cikkuṭu,
	ka(ṉṉaṭam)	-	avara, avari, āvarē, amarē;
	ma(laiyāḷam)	-	avara, amara;
	kō(ttam)	-	avar
	tuṭa(vam)	-	evir (f);
	tu(ḷuvam)	-	avare, apare, lablab vulgaris
			avaṭe. apaṭe. āvaṭe 
				(a kind of bean)
avaraikkāy eṉpatu malaiyāḷattil avarakka eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. amarakka eṉpatu avarakka eṉpataṉ tirivu.
tuḷuc coṟkaḷuḷ rakaramuḷḷavai oruvakaiyaiyum ṭakara muḷḷavai maṟṟoru vakaiyaiyum kuṟippaṉavākap parō emaṉō tiraviṭa akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tuḷu akaramutaliyil aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkappaṭa villai.
iṇaimoḻi
avarai tuvarai.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
avarai koyyuna rāra māntum  (puṟam 215:5)
ciṟukoṭik koḷḷē poṟikiḷa ravaraiyoṭu  (puṟam. 335:5)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷ, paṇṭaik kālattil avaraiyum kāṭṭil, moccaipōla viḷaikkappaṭṭa teṉṟō, moccaiyum  avaraiyeṉap paṭṭateṉṟō, karuta iṭantarum.
koṭṭaiyavarai yeṉṟu patārtta kuṇa cintāmaṇi kuṟittiruppatu. oru taṉi vakaiyākat teriyavillai.
cīrtiruttam:
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, kōḻi yavaraiyai vāḷavarai (sword-bean) yeṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷatu.
ārāl mīṉavarai, āṉaikkātavarai, koḻuppavarai, cuṭalai avarai mutaliya peyarkaḷ atiṟ kuṟikkappeṟavillai.
mēl nāṭṭut tamiḻaṟiñar tam aṟiyāmaiyāl avaraikkāy eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa avarakka, amarakka eṉṉum coṟkaḷaiyum avaraiyaik kuṟikkum taṉic coṟkaḷākak kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
aviyuṇavum ceviyuṇavum
māntaṉ uyirvāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta kāṟṟu, nīr, uṇavu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉuḷ, mutalatu uṭal nalattiṟkēṟṟa iṭatti lellām irukku miṭattilēyē muyaṟciyiṉṟi ilavacamākap peṟakkūṭiyatu; iṭaiyatu pañca millāta kālamellām ciṟu muyaṟciyāṟ peṟakkūṭiyatu; iṟutiyatē viḷaivukkāla mel lām muyaṟciyiṉālō, vilaikoṭuttō peṟakkūṭiya tākum.
nākarika vāḻkkaikku vēṟu pala poruḷkaḷum vēṇṭu māyiṉum, uyir vāḻkkaikku vēṇṭiyatē iṉṟiyamaiyāta tātaliṉ, māntaṉ kavalaippaṭṭut tēṭa vēṇṭiya mutaṉmai yāṉa poruḷ uṇavoṉṟē.
uṇaviṉālēyē uyir uṭampil niṟpatuṭaṉ, uṭampum uriya vaḷarcciyaṭaikiṉṟatu. uṇaviṉ māṟṟamē uṭampu. uṭampiṉ naṉṉilaiyē uyir nilai.
nīriṉ ṟamaiyā yākkaik kellām
uṇṭi koṭuttō ruyirkoṭut tōrē
uṇṭi mutaṟṟē yuṇaviṉ piṇṭam
uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē
nīrum nilaṉum puṇari yōrīṇ(ṭu)
uṭampu muyirum paṭaittici ṉōrē.  (puṟam.18)
uṇavu peṟutaṟkuriya palvēṟu toḻilkaḷaip payilvataṟkum, avaṟṟaic ceytaṟkum, uriya paruvattil maṇam purivataṟkum, piṉṉar makkaḷaip peṟṟu vaḷarttaṟkum, nīṇṭa kālam uṭampiṉ naṉṉilai vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, ulaka vāḻkkaiyai virumpuvōrellām uṭampaip pēṇuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyāta tākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, paṇpaṭṭa makkaḷ immai vāḻkkaikku maṭṭumaṉṟi maṟumai vāḻkkaik kum aṟivupeṟa vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, ataṉ poruṭṭum uṭampiṉ nīḷ naṉṉilai iṉṟiyamaiyāta tākum.
uṭampār aḻiyiṉ uyirār aḻivar
tiṭampaṭa meyññāṉañ cēravu māṭṭār
uṭampai vaḷarkkum upāyam aṟintē
uṭampai vaḷarttēṉ uyirvaḷart tēṉē.  (724)
uṭampiṉai muṉṉam iḻukkeṉ ṟiruntēṉ
uṭampiṉuk kuḷḷē uṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē uttamaṉ kōyilkoṇ ṭāṉeṉ(ṟu)
uṭampiṉai yāṉirun tōmpukiṉ ṟēṉē.  (725)
eṉpavai tirumūlar tirumantiraṅkaḷ.
eṉavē, māntaṉ irumai vāḻviṟkum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu uṭampu eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ittakaiya uṭampai uruvākku vatum iṟutivarai nilaip paṭuttuvatum uṇavē.
uṇavai viḷaippatu uḻavuttoḻil. attoḻilai makkaḷ aṉaivarum mēṟkoḷḷiṉum, iru nilaimaiyāl uṇavut taṭṭu ēṟpaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu maḻaiyiṉmai; iṉṉoṉṟu makkaṭ perukkam.
maḻaiyiṉmai nīṭikkātu; ulakam oruṅkē aḻiyum ūḻiyiṟuti varai, pār muḻutum paravavuñ ceyyātu. ātalāl, pañcakālattil vaḷanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vaṟaṭci nāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya uṇavai varuvittuk koḷḷalām. āyiṉ makkaṭ perukkattāl ēṟpaṭum uṇavuttaṭṭu nilaittatum nīkka muṭiyātatumākum. maḻai ērāḷamākap peyyiṉum uṇavupporuḷ viḷaivatu nilamē yātalālum, makkaṭ perukkattiṟkēṟpa nilamum virivuṟāmai yālum, nilavarampīṭum pakirntuṇṭalum, kuṭumpa maṭṭuppāṭum araciyalēṟpāṭāka amaikiṉṟaṉa. ivvakai araciyalē kūṭṭuṭaimai (Socialism) eṉpatu.
kūṭṭuṭaimai eṉṉum āṭcimuṟai ikkālattatēyāṉālum, ataṉ uyirnāṭik kūṟāṉa pakirntuṇṭalai, ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuvar.
pāttūṇ marīi yavaṉaip paciyeṉṉum
tīppiṇi tīṇṭa varitu.  (227)
pakuttuṇṭu palluyi rōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellān talai.  (322)
tammi liruntu tamatupāt tuṇṭaṟṟāl
ammā arivai muyakku  (1107)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷmaṇikaḷāl neṟiyiṭṭuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
avar kālattil makkaṭ perukkamillai; nilakkuṟaivumillai. avai yiruntiruppiṉ.
ilameṉ ṟacaii yiruppāraik kāṇiṉ
nilameṉṉum nallāḷ nakum  (1040)
eṉṟu kūṟiyirār.
ākavē, avar ikkālattil iruntārāyiṉ, nila varampīṭṭiṟkum, kuṭumpak kaṭṭuppāṭṭut tiṭṭattiṟkum neṟi vakuttēyiruppār.
avar iṉṟu illāviṭiṉum, avar pōṅkilēyē, intiyat talaimai mantiriṉiyārāṉa tirumāṭṭiyār intirākānti yammaiyār, irupāṉ kuṟippup poruḷāṭcit tiṭṭattai vakuttiruppatu, intiyar mītu iṟaivaṉ koṇṭa pēraruḷeṉṟē karututal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷ āṟaṟivu paṭaitta uyartiṇai eṉṉum uyarnta uyiriṉattaic cērntavarātalāl. puṟavuṭampiṟkuriya aviyuṇavai maṭṭumaṉṟi akavuṭampiṟkuriya aṟivuṇavaiyum arumpāṭu paṭṭēṉum tēṭi nukartal vēṇṭum. aviyuṇaveṉṟatu avitta allatu camaitta uṇavai. nākarika makkaḷuṇavu avikkappaṭṭatē eṉpataic colla vēṇṭuvatillai. aṟivuṇavu cevivāyilākak kēṭṭu uṭkoḷḷap paṭutalāl ceviyuṇa veṉappaṭṭatu.
cevivā yāka neñcukaḷa ṉākak
kēṭṭavai kēṭṭavai viṭātuḷat tamaittu  (naṉ. 40)
eṉṟu naṉṉūṟ pāyiraṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.
aviyuṇavai maṭṭum oruvaṉ virumpuvāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ uyar tiṇaiyaic cērntavaṉākāṉ. uṭampāl uyartiṇaiyāyiṉum uḷattāl aḵṟiṇai yākavē karutappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉāl makkaḷiṉattiṟkē iḻivu uṇṭākum. ataṉāl,
cevikkuṇa villāta pōḻtu ciṟitu
vayiṟṟukkum īyap paṭum.  (412)
eṉṟu ceviyuṇaviṉ perum ciṟappai eṭuttukkūṟi.
ceviyiṟ cuvaiyuṇarā vāyuṇarviṉ mākkaḷ
aviyiṉum vāḻiṉum eṉ.  (420)
eṉṟu ceviyuṇavu koḷḷātavaṉai iṟappa iḻittum paḻittum kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
ātalāl, tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum, āriyattiṟku mūlamumāṉa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyākiya oppuyarvaṟṟa tamiḻai, tāy moḻiyākak koṇṭiruntum, ataṉaic caṟṟum pēṇātu. tamiḻ vantu vayiṟṟai niṟaikkumā eṉṟu puṟakkaṇittuk kūṟuvatu, taṉṉait tamiḻaṉeṉṟu collik koḷpavaṉukkuc caṟṟum takātu. ceviyuṇavuṇṭu koṇṭē aviyuṇavum peṟa iyalumpōtu. oruvaṉ ēṉ tamiḻait taḷḷa vēṇṭum? tamiḻait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻariṭait tamiḻiṟ pēcittāṉē vāḻavoṇṇum! atu karutiyēṉum kūṭṭuṭaimaik koḷkaiyaṉ tamiḻaip pōṟṟuka. tāṉ pōṟṟāviṭiṉum pōṟṟuvārai iyaṉṟavarai pōṟṟuka.  (teṉmoḻi kumpam 1976.)
aviḻ
aviḻ = ventu malarnta cōṟṟup parukkai, cōṟu.
aviḻ - aviḻtu - aviḻtam - amiḻtam = uṇavu
aṟucuvai nālvakai amiḻtam  (maṇi 28:116.)
aviḻtu - amiḻtu - amutu = cōṟu, uṇavu, nīr.
nīrum uṇavātalāl amuteṉap peṟṟatu.
amiḻtam - amutam = cōṟu, nīr.
marumam - (mammam) - ammam = mulai, tāyppāl, kuḻantai yuṇavu  (ti.ma. 42.)
pālum oruvakai yuṇavātalālum, ammam amutu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ orupuṭai oppumaiyiṉālum, amutu eṉṉum collum pālaik kuṟittatu. amutu = pāl. amutam, pāl, amiḻtu = pāl.  (ti.ma.43.)
avai - capā (sabha#) - i.vē.
amaital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal. amai = kūṭṭam.
amai -avai o.nō. ammai - avvai, kumi - kuvi.
avaiyaṭakkiyal avaiyalkiḷavi eṉpaṉa, tolkāppiyarkku muṉpē toṉṉūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkivanta ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷām.
vāyuṟai vāḻttē avaiyaṭak kiyalē
ceviyaṟi vuṟūuveṉa avaiyum aṉṉa.  (tol. 1367)
avaiyal kiḷavi maṟaittaṉar kiḷattal.  (tol. 925)
avai - avaiyam.
tantai makaṟkāṟṟu naṉṟi yavaiyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.  (kuṟaḷ 67)
avai - cavai. o.nō. iḷai - ciḷai, utai - cutai.
cavai - capā (va.)
vaṭacol vaṭiviṟkup piṉvarumāṟu mūvēṟu vakaiyil mūlaṅkāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.)
(1) sa = saha (kūṭa). sa-sapā = kūṭṭam.
(2) pā (bha#) = viḷaṅku (to shine). sapā = viḷakkattōṭu (pirakācattōṭu) kūṭiyatu, aṟiñaravai.
ipporuḷiṟ pā eṉṉum vaṭacol pāl eṉṉum teṉ colloṭu toṭarpuṭaiyatāyirukkalām.
pāl = veṇmai. pāṟṟiru nīṟṟem paramaṉai tiruvāca (446).
(3) sip.
itu uṭaṉpiṟappaik kuṟikkum mēlaiyāriyat tiyūttāṉiyac col. ākkacuppōrṭuc ciṟṟakaramutaliyil (C.O.D.) piṉvarumāṟu kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
sib = a brother or sister. sibship = the group of children from the same two  parents.
OE sib (b), MDu sib (be), OHG sippi, Goth sibjis.
im mūvakai mūlattiṉum tamiḻmūlamē poruttamāyiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 80-81)
avai : amaital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal. amai = kūṭṭam, ampalam, amai - avai. o.nō. ammai - avvai, kumi - kuvi.
avai - cavai, o.nō. amaiyam - camaiyam, eṭṭi - ceṭṭi. avai - avaiyam. am oru perumaip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu: nilai nilaiyam, kampu - kampam, cavai - capā (va.).  (ti.ma. 737.)
aḻintupōṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ
ēṉaiya moḻikaḷilellām ilakkiyam varavara uyarntum mikuntum varavum, tamiḻilō varavarat tāḻntum kuṟaintum vantirukkiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yilakkiyaṅkaḷ iru peruṅkaṭalkaḷākat toṉṉūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭamoḻikkaṭal muṉṉuḷḷapaṭiyē iṉṟum kuṟaiyātuḷatu. āṉāl, teṉmoḻikkaṭalō oru ciṟu kuḷamāka vaṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
akattiyarukku muntiya tamiḻnūlkaḷil, ceṅkōṉtaraic celavu eṉṉum ciṟiya nūliṉ oru pakutiyē iṉṟu kiṭaittuḷatu.
talaikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa ettuṇaiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāraiyum mutukurukum, kaḷariyāviraiyumeṉa ittoṭak kattaṉavum iṭaikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa kaliyum, kurukum, veṇṭāḷiyum, viyāḻamālaiyakavalumeṉa it toṭakkat taṉavum kaṭaikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa paripāṭaluṭpala vum, kūttum variyum ciṟṟicaiyum, pēricaiyum ippōtillai.
talaikkaḻakakkālat tilakkaṇamākiya akattiyamum, iṭaikkaḻakat tilakkaṇa mākiya māpurāṇamum icainūlum pūta purāṇamum ippōtillai.
iṉi, aṭi nūl, aṇiyiyal, avinayam, avinantamālai, āciriya mālai, āciriyamuṟi, āṉantaviyal, iḷantiraiyam, intira kāḷiyam, aintiram, ōviyanūl, kaṭakaṇṭu, kaṇakkiyal, kaliyāṇa kātai, kaḷavu nūl, kavimayakkiṟai, kalaikkōṭṭut taṇṭu, kālakēci, kākkaipāṭiṉiyam, kuṇṭalakēci, kuṇanūl, kōḷ nūl, caṅkayāppu, cayantam, cintam, caccapuṭaveṇpā, cātavākaṉam, ciṟpanūl, ciṟukākkaipāṭiṉiyam, ciṟukurīiyurai, cuttāṉantap pirakācam, ceyaṉmuṟai, ceyiṟṟiyam, tantiravākkiyam, tumpippāṭṭu, takaṭūr yāttirai, tāḷacamuttiram, tāḷavakai yōttu, tēcikamālai, nākakumārakāviyam, nīlakēci, pañca pāratīyam, paratam, pañcamarapu, patiṉāṟupaṭalam, paratacēṉā patiyam, parinūl, palkāppiyam, palkāyam, paṉmaṇimālai, paṉṉiru paṭalam, pāvaippāṭṭu, pāṭṭiyaṉ marapu. pāṭṭu maṭai, pāratam (peruntēvaṉār iyaṟṟiyatu), puṇarppāvai, putaiyal nūl, purāṇacākaram, periyapammam, peruvallam, pōkkiyam, maṇiyāram, mativāṇar nāṭakattamiḻ nūl, mantira nūl, mayēccuraryāppu, mārkkaṇṭēyar kāñci, muṟuval, muttoḷḷāyiram, mūppeṭṭuc ceyyuḷ, mūvaṭi muppatu, mōtirappāṭṭu, yacōtarakāviyam, vaccattoḷḷāyiram, vañcippāṭṭu, vaḷaiyāpati, vāyppiyam, viḷakkattār kūttu mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta nūlkaḷ kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilum piṟ kālattilumiruntavai yippōtillai.
pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻppāratam, tiruccirāppaḷḷiyantāti, srīrāja rāja vijayam, nāṭakanūl, vīraṇukka vijayam, kulōttuṅka cōḻa caritai, aṣṭātaca purāṇam, aruṇilai vicākaṉ tamiḻp pāratam, peruvañci, attikirit tirumāl cintu, kāṅkēyaṉ piḷḷaikkavi, vīramālai, tiruvatikaik kalampakam. tiruvallai yantāti mutaliyavai, ituvaraiyaṟiyappaṭāta nūlkaḷākac cāsaṉat tamiḻkkavi caritattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭaikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa pulavarkaḷiyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷil pala ippōtu kiṭaikkavillai. oṭṭakkūttariyaṟṟiya arumpait toḷḷāyiram, etirnūl mutaliyavai iṉṉum veḷivaravillai.
iṭaikkaḻaka virukkaiyākiya kapāṭapurattil, eṇṇāyirat teccam nūl kaḷiruntu piṉpu kaṭalkōḷuṇṭaḻintaṉaveṉ ṟoru vaḻimuṟai vaḻakkuḷḷatu.
paṟpala kalaikaḷ paṇṭait tamiḻiliruntu piṉpaḻintu pōyiṉa veṉpatai.
ēraṇa muruvam yōkam icaikaṇak kiratam cālam
tāraṇa maṟamē cantam tampanīr nilamu lōkam
māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇaṅ koṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyaru māḷa
eṉṉum ceyyuḷālaṟiyalām.
mēṟkūṟiya nūlkaḷellām aḻintu pōṉamaikkuk kaṭal kōḷkaḷum, āriyattiṉāl tamiḻar uyarnilaikkalvi yiḻantamaiyum, iruperuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷākum.
tolkāppiyam oṉṟil vallavarē oru perum pulavarāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟār. tolkāppiyattiṟkuc camamum atiṉuñ ciṟantavumāṉa ettuṇaiyō nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.  (o.mo.)
aḻipaci
kolvatu pōla varuttuvataṉālum kuṭippiṟappu kalvi māṉam aṟivuṭaimai mutaliya pēṟukaḷaiyum, paṇpukaḷaiyum aḻip pataṉālum kaṭumpaci aḻipaciyām.  (ti.ma. 136.)
aḻukkāṟu
aḻuṅkutal varuntutal allatu tuṉpuṟutal. aḻuṅkuvatu aḻukku. atu uṟu eṉṉum tuṇaiviṉai peṟṟu aḻukkuṟu eṉa niṟkum. aḻukkuṟutal, piṟarākkam kaṇṭu poṟātu varun tutal. nācamuṟu eṉṉum viṉai nācamaṟu eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkil tirintāṟ pōṉṟu aḻukkuṟu eṉpatum aḻukkaṟu eṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkil tirintatu. aḻukkaṟ ṟakaṉṟārum illai (170) eṉa vaḷḷuvarē kūṟutal kāṇka. nācamuṟṟup pōvāṉ eṉpatu nācamaṟṟup pōvāṉ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.  (ti.ma.102.)
aḷ
aḷ-ala (muḷ) - va.
o.nō. nuḷ - naḷ - naḷḷi = naṇṭu. naḷ-naḷi = tēḷ. vaṭamoḻi yil ali eṉṉuñ col tēḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.  (va.va.82.)
aḷakam
aḷakam - alaka - aḷ = ceṟivu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital. aḷḷal = nerukkam. aḷam = ceṟivu. aḷam - aḷakam = 1. peṇmayir. (piṅ), ceṟinta kūntal. 2. mayirk kuḻaṟci (piṅ.).  (va.va.81.)
aḷavaikaḷ
paḻantamiḻāṭciyiṟ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa aḷavaikaḷ, perum pālum ikkālattuḷḷa tamiḻ aḷavaikaḷē. avai eṇṇal, eṭuttal, mukattal, nīṭṭal eṉa nālvakaippaṭum.
(1) eṇṇalaḷavai: eṇṇalaḷavai, ciṟṟilakkam pērilakkam eṉa iruvakai. arai kāl araikkāl vīcam (mākāṇi) mutaliya piṉṉaveṇkaḷ ciṟṟilakkamum, oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu mutaliya muḻuveṇkaḷ pērilakkamum ākum. ciṟṟilakkattil, kīḻvāyilakkameṉṟum mēlvāyilakkameṉṟum iruvakai yuṇṭu. kīḻarai (1/640), kīḻkkāl (1/1280), kīḻaraikkāl (1/2560), kīḻvīcam (1/5120), mutaliyaṉa kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakkamum, mēlarai (arai). mēṟkāl (kāl), mēlaraikkāl (araikkāl) mēlvīcam (vīcam) mutaliyaṉa mēlvāyc ciṟṟilakkamumākum. kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakkattoṭu oppu nōkkiyē, arai, kāl  araikkāl mutaliyaṉa mēlvāyc ciṟṟilakkameṉappaṭum. mēlvāyc ciṟṟilakkattil aṭimaṭṭa eṇ mēl muntiri eṉṉum muntiri (1/320) yum, kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakkattil aṭimaṭṭa eṇ kīḻmuntiri (1/102.400) yum, ākum.
(2) eṭuttalaḷavai: eṭuttal eṉpatu eṭuttu niṟuttal. eṭutta laḷavaiyil, poṉṉum maṇiyum niṟukka oṉṟum, piṟa poruḷ kaḷai niṟukka oṉṟumāka. iruvakaiyuṇṭu. poṉṉiṟai yaḷavaikkup poṉṉilakkam eṉṟu peyar. poṉ ērāḷamā yiruppiṉ, atuvum piṟa poruḷpōl niṟukkappaṭum.
kīḻvaruvatu poṉṉaḷavai :
4 nel 		-	1 kuṉṟimaṇi
2 kuṉṟimaṇi 	-	1 mañcāṭi
2 mañcāṭi 	-	1 vallam (paṇattūkkam)
10 vallam 	-	1 kaḻañcu (16 avuṉcu)
kīḻvaruvatu piṟaporuḷaḷavai
4 kaḵcu 		-	1 palam (toṭi)
8 palam 		-	1 cēr
5 cēr 		-	1 vīcai
2½ vīcai	 -	1 tulām (kā, tūkku, niṟai)
8 vīcai 		-	1 maṇaṅku
20 maṇaṅku 	-	1 kaṇṭi (pāram)
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil 5 vīcai koṇṭatu oru tulāmāka viruntatu. araca muttiraiyiṭṭa aḷavukkal, kuṭiñaikkal, pāṭikkal, paṇṭārakkal eṉṉum peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, nakaraṅkaḷil atu nakarakkal eṉappaṭṭatu. pēraḷavāṉa poṉṉai niṟukka āṇikkal eṉṉum niṟaikal iruntatu. mikap pēraḷavāṉa poṉ tulām kaṇakkāka niṟukkappeṟṟatu.
(3) mukattalaḷavai: mukantaḷakkappaṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ, nel perumpāṉmaiyākavum ciṟantatākavumiruttal paṟṟi, mukatta laḷavai nellilakkam eṉappaṭum.
kīḻvaruvatu mukattalaḷavai :
2 ceviṭu 		-	piṭi
5 ceviṭu 		-	1 āḻākku
2 āḻākku	-	1 uḻakku
2 uḻakku 		-	1 uri
2 uri 			-	1 nāḻi
8 nāḻi 		-	1 kuṟuṇi (marakkāl)
2 kuṟuṇi 		-	1 patakku
2 patakku		-	1 tūṇi (kāṭi)
3 tūṇi		-	1 kalam
400 kuṟuṇi 	-	1 karicai (paṟai)
araca muttiraiyiṭṭa aḷavai nāḻiyum marakkālum, araca paṇṭārattil aracaṉ peyaraiyum, kōyiṟ paṇṭārattil teyvap peyaraiyum tāṅkiyiruntaṉa. cōḻāntakaṉ nāḻi aruṇmoḻittēvaṉ marakkāl eṉpaṉa aracaṉ peyaraiyum, āṭavallāṉ marakkāl ceppukkāl tirucciṟṟampalamuṭai yāṉ marakkāl eṉpaṉa teyvap peyaraiyum, tāṅkiyavai yākum.
iṉi, ovvoru nāṭṭiṟkum ciṟappāṉa perumukattalaḷavum uṇṭu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil kōṭṭai (21 marakkāl) eṉpatum vaṭacōḻanāṭṭil puṭṭi (40 marakkāl) eṉpatum perumukatta laḷavām.
(4) nīṭṭalaḷavai : nīṭṭalaḷavai, vaḻiyaḷavaiyum nilavaḷavai yum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ nilavaḷavai kuḻik kaṇakku eṉappaṭum.

cey eṉṟu oru nila aḷavu caṅkakālattiliruntatu.
nilavariyait tulliyamāy vitittaṟ poruṭṭu, cōḻap peru nāṭu muḻumaiyum, mutalām irācarācaṉ kālattil orumuṟai yum, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil oru muṟaiyum, mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil oru muṟaiyum aḷakkappaṭṭatu. av vaḷavaiyai naṭattiṉa atikāri ulakaḷantāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kaiyāṇṭa aḷavukōl ulakaḷanta kōl eṉappaṭṭatu.
ellai māṟumpōtellām vaḷanāṭukaḷum nāṭukaḷum aḷakkappeṟum. avaṟṟai yaḷakkuṅ kōl iṟaiyiṟukkuṅ kōl eṉappeṟum, kuṭi tāṅkik kōl eṉṟum ōr aḷavu kōl muṉkālattiruntatu.
nilaṅkaḷ mika nuṭpamāka aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu “āka iṟai yili nīṅku nilam mukkālē iraṇṭu mākkāṇi araikkāṇi muntirikaikkīḻ araiyē iraṇṭumā mukkāṇik kīḻ mukkālē nāṉkumā araikkāṇi muntiraik kīḻ nāṉ kumāviṉāl iṟai kaṭṭiṉa kāṇikkaṭaṉ” (cō., pak.58) eṉpataṉāl viḷaṅkum. itil kuṟikkappaṭṭa nila aḷavu 1/52,428,800,000 vēli. iṉi, kālavaḷavai varumāṟu :

aḷi
aḷi - ali
aḷ = kūrmai, nīrmuḷḷi. aḷ - aḷakam = paṉṟimuḷ. aḷ - aḷi = koṭṭum muḷḷuḷḷatu, tēṉī.  (va.va. 81.)
aṟakkaṭaṉ
oruvaṉ kaṭamaiyākac ceyyavēṇṭiya pala viṉaikaḷuḷ, aṟamum oṉṟākum, uyartiṇaiyaic cārnta kāraṇattiṉālum, ulakam kaṭavuḷait talaimaiyākak koṇṭa oru māperuṅ kuṭumpam eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉālum, ovvoruvaṉum piṟarkku, avaruḷḷum ciṟappāka ēḻai eḷiyavarkkut taṉṉāl iyaṉṟa varai utaviyum naṉmaiyum ceytal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuk kaṭappāṭu eṉṟu peyar.
kaimmāṟu vēṇṭā kaṭappāṭu mārimāṭ
ṭeṉṉāṟṟuṅ kollō vulaku  (kuṟaḷ - 211)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kaṭappāṭu eṉṉum colliṉ mutaṟporuḷ kaṭaṉ eṉpatē. ovvoruvarum aṟañceyya vēṇṭuvatu kaṭaṉ eṉṉum karut tiṉāl, accollukku oppuravoḻukal (upakārañ ceytal) eṉpatu vaḻip poruḷāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu.  (col. 111.)
aṟattoṭunilai
kātaliyiṉ melivu kaṇṭu, ataip pōkkuvataṟku, avaḷ peṟṟōr kaṭṭuvicci (kuṟikāri) vēlaṉ (mantirakkāraṉ) mutaliyōriṉ tuṇai vēṇṭum pōtō, ataṟku muṉṉatākavō, kātali tāṉākavēṉum taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavēṉum taṉ kātalaṉaippaṟṟit terivittal.  (ta.ti.6.)
aṟamum koṭaiyum
aṟam: aṟaneṟiyiṟ poruḷīṭṭi apporuḷāl aṟaneṟiyil iṉpan tuykka vēṇṭum eṉpatai uṇarttaṟkē, aṟanūlār immaipporuḷ mūṉṟaiyum aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṉum immuṟaippaṭuttik kūṟiṉar. ovvoruvaṉum aṟaneṟiyil taṉtaṉ toḻilaic ceyvatē, avaṉavaṉ poruḷīṭṭi iṉpantuykkum vaḻiyākum. aracaṉ toḻilāvatu nāṭukāval. pōrviṉaiyum nāṭukāvaluṭpaṭṭatē. ataṉāl akkālattu aracar, perumpālum, tam viṉaikaḷilellām aṟattaik kaṭaippiṭittaṉar.
kuṟaḷarum cintarum, araṇmaṉaiyil makaḷir uṟaiyum uvaḷaka meykāvalarāka amarttap peṟṟaṉar. tēvāram pāṭum kuruṭar patiṉaṟuvar, tiruvāmāttūrk kōyilil tēvārappāṭakarāka amarttap peṟṟiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam cila eccap piṟaviyarkku vāḻkkai vacati ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu.
ciṟuvar, meliyār, tūyar mutaliyōraik kollāmaiyum; porunilaiyaṟṟavaṉ, tōṟṟōṭupavaṉ, mutaliyōroṭu porāmaiyum; aṭaikkalam pukuntavaṉaik kaiviṭāmaiyum; pōraṟaṅkaḷākap perumpālum kaṭaippiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
paṇṭait tamiḻaracar, tam nāṭṭai allatu kuṭikaḷai āḷum toḻilai āṭci eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟiyātu, kāval puravu ōmpal mutaliya coṟkaḷālēyē kuṟikka virumpiṉar. avar kāval pūṇṭa iṭam. kuṭai niḻal eṉṟum aṭiniḻal eṉṟum, kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟa kōvalaṉaik koṉṟatāl taṉ kōl kōṭiṟṟeṉṟu kaṇṭa noṭiyē uyir tuṟappāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ aṟavuṇarcci ettuṇai aḷaviṟanta tāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum!
koṭai: akkālattaracar, aṟamnōkkiyum kalai vaḷarcci paṟṟiyum ūḻiyap pārāṭṭākavum, viṉaiyūkkaṟ poruṭṭum. palvēṟu koṭai nikaḻtti vantaṉar. avai, uṇṭikkoṭai, poṟkoṭai, ūrtikkoṭai, vilaṅkukkoṭai, ciṉṉak koṭai, peyarkkoṭai, nilakkoṭai, makaṟkoṭai, āṭcikkoṭai eṉa oṉpāṉ vakaippaṭum.
pārppaṉarkku nāḷtōṟum uṇavaḷikkumpaṭi, āṅkāṅku kōyilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum ūṭṭuppuraikaḷilum nitiyum māṉiyamum viṭṭiruntatu, uṇṭik koṭaiyākum. peruñ cōṟṟutiyañcēralātaṉ pāratap pōrppaṭai kaḷiraṇṭiṟkum patiṉeṭṭunāḷum peruñcōṟu vaḻaṅkiyatum, uṇṭik koṭaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
pulavarkkaḷitta pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa poṟkācum, pāṇark kaḷitta poṟṟāmaraippūvum, pāṭiṉiyarkkum viṟaliyarkkum aḷitta poṉṉari mālaiyum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiya pāṇarkkum kaṇikaiyarkkum aḷitta āyiratteṇkaḻañcu poṉṉum. (muṭṭil pāṇarum āṭiyaṉ makaḷirum eṭṭoṭu puṇarntavāyirampoṉ peṟupa eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallārurai mēṟkōḷ (cilap. pak. 121) veṟṟiviḻāt toṭar pāka maṟaiyōrkkaḷitta tulāpāram (cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ māṭalamaṟaiyōṉukkaḷitta tulāpāram 50 tulām (375 pavuṇṭu) poṉṉākum.) eṉṉum āḷniṟaip poṉṉum iraṇiya karuppam eṉṉum poṟkuṭamum, poṟkoṭai yākum.
pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliyōrkkaḷitta kutirai yāṉai tēr. eṉpavai ūrtikkoṭaiyākum.
maṟaiyōrk kavvappōtaḷitta āvāyiram (kōcakaram) vilaṅkuk koṭaiyākum.
avaikkaḷat talaimaip pulavarkkaḷitta kuṭai koṭi civikai mutaliya virutukaḷum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiyavarkkaḷitta talaikkōlum, vaṇikat talaivarkkaḷitta eṭṭippūvum, paṭait talaivarkkaḷitta ēṉāti mōtiramum, amaiccark kaḷitta kāvitip pūvum. ciṉṉakkoṭaiyākum.
uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāḷarkkaḷitta vēḷ aracu eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷum, vaṇikar talaivarkkaḷitta eṭṭip paṭṭamum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiyavarkkaḷitta talaikkōṟ paṭṭamum, amaiccarkkaḷitta kāvitip paṭṭamum, paṭaittalaivark kaḷitta ēṉātip paṭṭamum,  palavakai araciyalatikārikaṭkaḷitta araiyaṉ (rāyaṉ) māvaraiyaṉ (mārāyaṉ) pēraraiyaṉ viḻuppēraraiyaṉ mūvēnta vēḷāṉ mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum, ciṟṟaracarkkaḷitta piḷḷai makkaḷ nāyaṉār eṉṉum paṭṭaṅ kaḷum, peyarkkoṭaiyākum. iṉi, aracar tam kuṭippeyar kaḷai amaiccar paṭaittalaivar mutaliyōrkkum, tam ciṟappup peyarkaḷaic ciṟṟaracarkkum paṭṭappeyarākavum peyaraṭai yākavum iṭuvatumuṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ paṭait talaivaṉ villavaṉkōtai eṉṟum, arimarttaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ mutalamaiccarāṉa māṇikkavācakar teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉṟum, mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉiṉ ciṟṟaracaruḷ oruvaṉ kulōttuṅkac cōḻap pirutikaṅkaṉ eṉṟum, peyarpeṟṟiruntamai kāṇka.
araciyal viṉaiñar, porunar (pōr maṟavar), potu nala vūḻiyar, pulavar, kalaiñar, vaṇikar, maṟaiyōr, aṟaccālai, maṭam, kōyil  mutaliyōrkku aḷitta nilam nilakkoṭai yākum. atu muṟṟūṭṭu, iṟaiyili, iṟai nilam, nilaviṟai ākiya nālvakai nilaimaiyilum; nilam ūr nāṭu eṉṉum mūvakaiyaḷavilum koṭukkappaṭṭatu. oru vakai variyumillātatu muṟṟūṭṭu. atu uṟāvarai, kācu koḷḷā viṟaiyili eṉavum paṭum. araciṟaimaṭṭum nīṅkiyatu  iṟaiyili. iṟaiyiṟukkum nilam iṟainilam. nilattil varum iṟaiyaimaṭṭum nukarvatu nilaviṟai.
araciyal viṉaiñarkku, akkālattil campaḷam umpaḷam eṉ ṉum iruvakaiyil vēlaippayaṉ aḷikkappaṭṭatu. nellāka vēṉum kācākavēṉum koṭuppatu campaḷam; nilamākak koṭuppatu umpaḷam. umpaḷam māṉiyam eṉappaṭum. atu nilaiyāṉatu; perumpālum iṟaiyiliyāyiruppatu.
māṉiyavakai: araciyal viṉaiñaruḷ, ūrttalaivaṉukkuk koṭuppatu ampalamāṉiyam; ūrkkaṇakkaṉukkuk koṭuppatu kaṇakkakkāṇi; veṭṭiyāṉukkuk koṭuppatu veṭṭippēṟu.
araciyal viṉaiñaruḷ orucārārāṉa porunaruḷ. paṭait talaivaṉukkuk koṭuppatu amaranāyakam; pōriṟpaṭṭavaṉ makaṉukku allatu maṉaivikkuk koṭuppatu irattakkāṇi (irattak kāṇikkai, irattamāṉiyam, utirappaṭṭi).
potunalavūḻiyaruḷ, kuṭimakkaṭkuk koṭuppatu kuṭimakkaḷ māṉiyam; paṟaiyaṭippavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu paṟaittuṭaivai; uvaccaṉukkuk koṭuppatu uvaccakkāṇi; maruttuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu maruttuvappēṟu; naccumaruttuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu viṭakara (viṣa hara) pōkam; ēriyuṭaippai aṭaittavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu ērippaṭṭi. ivaiyellāvaṟṟiṟkum potuppeyar ūḻiyamāṉiyam eṉpatu. ivaṟṟuṭ pala, ūravaiyārāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭavaiyēṉum, aracavoppam peṟṟavai.
vaṇikaruṭ ciṟanta toṇṭu ceytavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṭṭip puravu.
pulavarkkuk koṭuppatu pulavarmuṟṟūṭṭu.
kalaiñaruḷ, naṭṭuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu naṭṭuvanilaikkāṇi; kaṇiyaṉukkuk koṭuppatu kaṇimuṟṟūṭṭu.
maṟaiyōr eṉṉum pirāmaṇarukkuk koṭuppatu piramakāyam (piramatēyam). atu tokutippaṭṭavarukkuk koṭuppatum taṉippaṭṭavarkkuk koṭuppatum eṉa iruvakai. akarappaṟṟu, akkiracālaippuṟam, akkirakāra vāṭai eṉpaṉa tokutip paṭṭavarkkuriyaṉa. pārppaṉar maṭṭum kuṭiyirukkum iṟaiyili nilam akarappaṟṟu. pārppaṉar perumpāṉmaiyākavum piṟar ciṟupāṉmaiyākavum kuṭiyirukkum iṟaiyilinilam akkirakāra vāṭai. pārppaṉarai uṇpikkum akkiracālaikku viṭappaṭṭa māṉiyam akkiracālaippuṟam. vētavirutti, paṭṭa virutti, attiyayaṉavirutti (attiyayaṉāṅkam), tayittiriyak kiṭaippuṟam, pāratappuṟam mutaliyaṉa taṉippaṭṭavarkkuriyaṉa.
cattiram, aṭiciṟcālai (ūṭṭuppurai, cōṟṟaṭaippu, aṉṉacattiram) mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku viṭum māṉiyam cālaippuṟam (cālāpōkam), aṭiciṟcālaikku maṭṭum viṭappaṭuvatu aṭiciṟpuṟam. taṇṇīrppantalukku viṭappaṭuvatu taṇṇīrp paṭṭi.
maṭattiṟku viṭappaṭuvatu maṭappuṟam.
kōyiṟku viṭappaṭuvatu tēvatāṉam allatu tēvatāṉappaṟṟu (tēvatāyam, tirunāmattukkāṇi, tiruviṭai yāṭṭam, tiru viṭaippaṟṟu). kuṭiyirukkum makkaḷoṭu cērttuk kōyiṟ kaḷikkappaṭum nilam kuṭi nīṅkāt tiruviṭaiyāṭṭam eṉap paṭum. piṭārikōyiṟku viṭappaṭum māṉiyam piṭāripaṭṭi allatu piṭāriviḷākam eṉṟum, camaṇapauttap paḷḷikaṭku viṭappaṭuvatu paḷḷiccantam eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
aṟamnōkkiyum matam paṟṟiyum viṭappaṭum māṉiyamellām potuvāka aṟappuṟam eṉappeṟum.
pārppaṉarkkum kōyiṟkum oru muḻuvūraittāṉañ ceyyum pōtu, oru peṇyāṉaiyai avvūraiccuṟṟip pōkaviṭṭu, atupōṉavaḻiyē ellaiyamaittuk kalluṅkaḷḷiyum naṭṭi, nīraṭṭik koṭuttu, perum paṇaikkāraṉ eṉṉum atikāri vāyilāy, ceppēṭṭil aracāṇai (āṇatti) poṟippatu marapu. peṇ yāṉaiyaiviṭṭu ellaiyamaittal piṭicūḻntu paṭākai naṭattal eṉappaṭum. tāṉañ ceyyappaṭum ūriluḷḷa uḻutuṇpār kārāṇmai eṉṉuñ collālum, uḻuvit tuṇpār mīyāṭci eṉṉuñ collālum, kuṟikkap peṟuvar. iṉi, ōr ūrai mēṟpārkkuṅ kūṭṭamum mīyāḷuṅ kaṇam eṉappaṭum. nīraṭṭik koṭutta ceppēṭṭut tāṉa mellām aṭṭippēṟu eṉap paṭṭaṉa.
kōyiṟku viṭappaṭum nilamellām iṟaiyiliyāka irukka vēṇṭiyi runtamaiyiṉ, aracaṉ tāṉāyc ceyyāta tēvatāṉa mellām avaṉuṭaiya icaivupeṟṟē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyu mpōtu, iṟai nīṅkalāl araciyaṟku ēṟpaṭum iḻappiṟku īṭāka, tāṉañ ceypavaṉ allatu ceypavar oru kuṟitta tokaiyaip paṇṭārattiṟ cērkkumpaṭi ūravai yāriṭam koṭuttu viṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
oru nilam allatu ūr iṟaiyiliyākkappaṭum pōtu, ataṉ iṟai nīkkam iṟaṅkal eṉṟum, atai iṟaiyilip pativup puttakattil peyartteḻututal nīṅkal allatu nāṭṭu nīṅkal eṉṟum, kuṟikkap peṟum. varikkaṇakkaiyum nilaṅkaḷiṉ nilai maiyaiyum entac camayattilum terintukoḷḷaṟ poruṭṭu, palvēṟu kaṇakkukaḷ vaikkappaṭṭiruntamaiyiṉ, cila camayaṅ kaḷil ōr iṟaiyili āṇaikku aimpatiṉmarukku mēṟpaṭṭa atikārikaḷ kainnāṭṭiṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. iṟutiyil aracaṉ iṭum muṭivāṉa āṇai kaṭaiyīṭu eṉap paṭṭatu.
nāṭṭuk kuḻappattiṉāl iḻakkappaṭṭa māṉiyavurimaiyai, kuḻappam nīṅkiyapiṉ cāṉṟukāṭṭi mīṇṭum peṟṟukkoḷḷa akkālattu iyalkai (cāttiyam) iruntatu. kaḷappāḷar āṭciyil iḻakkappaṭṭa vēḷvikkuṭip piramatēya vurimai, iru nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup  piṉ mīḷa nāṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭiya vāṭciyiṉ ēḻām talaimuṟaiyil putuppikkappeṟṟatu.
cēramāṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēlkeḻukuṭṭuvaṉ, paraṇarkkut taṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēralaikkoṭuttatu makaṟkoṭai yākum. makaṟkoṭaiyāvatu makaṉai māṇavaṉākak koṭuttal.
takaṭūreṟinta peruñ cēralirumpoṟai, aricil kiḻārkkut taṉ ariyaṇaiyaik koṭuttatu āṭcik koṭaiyākum. āyiṉ, avar atai ēṟkamaṟuttu avaṉiṭam amaiccup pūṇṭār.
koṭai maṭam : paṇṭaiyaracaruṭ palarkkuk koṭai oru ciṟanta kuṇamā yiruntapōtē, oruvakaiyiṟ kuṟṟamākavum iruntatu. atu koṭai maṭam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pēkaṉ ōr aruviṉai yuyarvilaik kaliṅkattai oru mayilmēṟ pōrttāṉ. pāri taṉ iḻaiyaṇi neṭuntērai oru mullaik koṭi paṭara niṟuttiyatoṭu, taṉakkirunta munnūṟūrkaḷaiyum paricilark kīntuviṭṭāṉ. imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ, taṉmēṟ pattuppāṭṭup pāṭiya kumaṭṭūrk kaṇṇaṉārkku, ainnūṟūr piramatāyamum muppatteṭṭiyāṇṭu teṉṉāṭṭuḷ varuvataṉiṟ pākamuṅ koṭuttāṉ. kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇinār muṭiccēral, kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉārkku, pattup pāṭṭiṟku nāṟpatilakkam poṉṉum tāṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamuṅ koṭut tāṉ. iṅṅaṉam, aḵṟiṇaiyuyirikaṭkut takāta poruḷ kaḷaiyum, aracu keṭumpaṭi paricilarkku nāṭu muḻuvataiyum, pala pulavar paciyāl vāṭa ōriru pulavarkku māperuñ celvat taiyum, aḷittatu koṭai maṭamām.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aṟiva (citta) matam
aṟivaṉ tēyam eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl. uruva vaṇakkattai (Idolatry) yoḻitta uyarnta aṟivamatamum paṇṭait tamiḻakattiliruntamai yuṇarappaṭum. piṟkālat tilirunta patiṉeṇṇaṟivarum tamiḻarē. kaṭavuḷ eṉṟa peyar ellāvaṟṟaiyuṅ kaṭanta muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
aṟivu
aṟivu eṉṉum col aṟital (Perception, understanding) aṟinta ceyti (knowledge), ōti (Wisdom) mati (Intelligence) eṉṉum nāṟporuḷ uṇarttum. - ti.ma.250.
aṟutoḻilōr
āpayaṉ kuṉṟum aṟutoḻilōr nūlmaṟappar
kāvalaṉ kālāṉ eṉiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 560)
ik kuṟaḷuraikku aṭimaṇaiyāyiruppatu aṟutoḻilōr eṉṉuñ col. ac coṟkup parimēlaḻakar antaṇar eṉṉuñ collāṟ pirāmaṇar eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟittu. aṟutoḻi lāvaṉa ōtal, ōtuvittal, vēṭṭal, vēṭpittal, ītal, ēṟṟaleṉa ivai. pacukkaḷ pāl kuṉṟiyavaḻi aviyiṉmaiyāṉum. atu koṭuttaṟkuriyār mantiraṅ kaṟpameṉpaṉa ōtāmaiyāṉum, vēḷvi naṭavātām. ākavē, vāṉam peyalollā teṉpatā yiṟṟu eṉṟu ataṟkēṟpa viḷakkavuraiyuṅ kūṟiṉār. ivvurai tavaṟāṉateṉpataṟkuk karaṇiyaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
1. nāṟpālārkkum aṟutoḻiluṇmai
antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷareṉṉum nālvakuppārk kum aṟutoḻil kūṟappaṭuvatāl, avaruḷ oru vakuppārai maṭṭum aṟutoḻilōr eṉṟu kaṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
ōta lōtuvittal vēṭṭal vēṭpittal
ītal ēṟṟal eṉṟiru mūvakai
ātik kālat tantaṇa raṟutoḻil
araca raṟutoḻil ōtal vēṭṭal
puraitīr perumpār purattal ītal
karaiyaṟu paṭaikkalaṅ kaṟṟal vicayam
vaṇika raṟutoḻil ōtal vēṭṭal
ītal uḻavu pacukkāval vāṇikam
vēḷāḷa raṟutoḻil uḻavupacuk kāval
teḷḷitiṉ vāṇikam kuyiluvam kārukaviṉai
oḷḷiya irupiṟap pāḷark kēvalceyal
kuyiluvam vāttiyaṅ koṭṭuta lākum
paruttinūl paṭṭunūl amaittāṭai yākkalum
cumattalum piṟavum kāruka viṉaittoḻil.
eṉṟu tivākaramum.
ōtalē yōtuvitta luṭaṉvēṭṭal vēṭpittiṭal
ītalē yēṟṟalāṟum ēṟkuman taṇartoḻiṟpēr
ōtalē vēṭṭalītal ulakōmpal paṭaipa yiṟṟal
mētaku pōrcey tīṭṭal vēntarcey toḻilka ḷāṟē
paṭaikuṭi kūḻamaiccup paṟṟiya naṭpiṉōṭu
neṭumati laraciyaṟku nikaḻttiyavāṟu pētam!
iṭaiyaṟa vōtal vēṭṭal vēḷāṇmai vāṇikatti
ṉuṭaṉirai kātta lērai yuḻalāṟum vaciyark kāmē
vaciyartan toḻilka ḷāṟuḷ vakuttamut toḻilka ḷāṉa
pacuvōmpal poruḷai yīṭṭal payiriṭal purāṇa māti
vacaitavi raṉukū lammām vakaittoḻil cūttirarkkām
eṉṟu cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭum. nālvakuppārkkum avvāṟu toḻil kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. parimēlaḻakar aracarkkum aṟutoḻil kūṟal
aṟaṉiḻukkā tallavai nīkki maṟaṉiḻukkā
māṉa muṭaiya taracu  (384)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaṭku.
taṉakkōtiya aṟattiṉ vaḻuvātoḻuki, aṟaṉallavai taṉṉāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum nikaḻāmaṟ kaṭintu, vīrattiṉ vaḻuvāta tāḻviṉmaiyiṉai yuṭaiyāṉ aracaṉ eṉṟu toṭarurai varaintu, ataṉ viḷakkattil, avvaṟamāvatu ōtal, vēṭṭal, ītaleṉṉum potut toḻiliṉum, paṭaikkalam payiṟal, palluyirōmpal, pakaittiṟan teṟutaleṉṉuñ ciṟapput toḻiliṉum, vaḻuvātu niṟṟal eṉṟu parimēlaḻakarē aracarkkum aṟutoḻil kūṟiyiruttalāl, aṟutoḻilōr eṉṉum potup peyarkkup pirāmaṇar eṉṉum poruḷum. nūlōtutal aracarkkum uriyatātalāl nūl maṟattal eṉpataṟku vēta mōtāmai eṉṉum poruḷum, poruntāmai yuṇarka.
3. tolkāppiyar aracaroḻinta mūvakuppārkku aṟutoḻil  kūṟal 
aṟuvakaip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum
aivakai marapiṉ aracar pakkamum
irumūṉṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr pakkamum  (tol poruḷ puṟat. 20)
eṉṟu, tolkāppiyar antaṇar (pirāmaṇar), vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum mūvakuppārkku aṟutoḻil kūṟiyiruppa tāl, avar  karuttuppaṭiyum aṟutoḻilōr eṉpatu avaruḷ oru vakuppārai maṭṭuñ ciṟappākac cuṭṭalākāmai aṟika. āriya muṟaippaṭi nūlōtalurimai vaṇikarkku muṇmaiyai nōkkuka.
4. aracarkku aṟutoḻiluṇmai
kōkkaḷaik kāttalum māpporu ḷīṭṭalum
ērttoḻil mūṉṟum vaiciyartan toḻilē
eṉṟu vaṇikarkku muttoḻil kūṟum piṅkalamum.
ōtal porutal ulaku purattal
ītal vēṭṭal paṭaipayiṟa laṟutoḻil
eṉṟu aracarkku aṟutoḻil kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vēṭṭal eṉpatu, ēmāṟṟu vakaiyil mūvēntariṭaip piṟ kālattup pukuttappaṭṭa āriya vaḻakkamātalāl, ataṟkup pakaramākat tamiḻaracarkkuriya toḻilākak koḷḷappaṭuvatu vēṭṭaiyāṭalē.
avicorin tāyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
uyircekut tuṇṇāmai naṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 259)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvaruṅ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
iṉi, iṟai (vari) kōṭal eṉpatu aracarkku iṉṟiyamaiyāta tātaliṉ, ataiyuñ cērppiṉ eḻutoḻilumākum. muṟai ceytal allatu taṇṭañceytal ulaku purattaluḷ aṭaṅkum; āyiṉ, porutal aṭaṅkātu. paḻaviṟal tāyattaik kāttuk koḷvataṟku maṭṭumaṉṟip putuviṟal tāyattai uṇṭākkik kōṭaṟkum, pōr iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉpatu paṇṭaik koḷkai.
5. pirāmaṇarkku aṟutoḻiliṉmai
tolkāppiyamum ellā nikaṇṭukaḷum pirāmaṇarkku aṟutoḻil kūṟiyiruppiṉum. aṟuvakaippaṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkam eṉṉun tolkāppiyat toṭarukku, āṟu kūṟṟiṉuṭ paṭṭa pārppiyaṟ kūṟu ... avai ōtal ōtuvittal vēṭṭal vēṭpittal koṭuttal kōṭal eṉa āṟām eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraittiruppiṉum, uṇmaiyiṟ pirāmaṇark kuriyavai ītaloḻinta aintoḻilē. itai, ōtal mutaliya ēṉai aintoḻiṟkum ciṟappuc ceyyuḷkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ītalukkum ītaṟciṟappiṟkum maṭṭum.
ilaṉeṉṟu mevva muraiyāmai yītal
kulaṉuṭaiyāṉ kaṇṇē yuḷa  (kuṟaḷ. 223)
īttuvakku miṉpa maṟiyārkol tāmuṭaimai
vaittiḻakkum vaṉka ṇavar  (kuṟaḷ 228)
eṉṟu potuc ceyyuḷkaḷaiyē eṭuttukkāṭṭiyirup patiṉiṉ ṟum,  viruntōmpalum vēḷāṇmaiyumillāp pirāmaṇar il vāḻkkaiyiṉiṉṟum. muṭṭipukum pārppār eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭiṉiṉ ṟum. aṟintukoḷka.
ōtal vēṭṭa lavaipiṟarc ceytal
īta lēṟṟaleṉ ṟāṟupurin toḻukum
aṟampuri yantaṇar vaḻimoḻin toḻuki  (patiṟṟup 24)
eṉpatu, āriya aṭimaiyāṉa palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭu   vaṉaip pālaik kautamaṉ eṉṉum pirāmaṇap pulavaṉ pāṭiyatātalāl. aḷavaic cāṉṟākātu. vētamōtuvitta laiyē ītaleṉṟu kūṟiṉ atu ōtuvittaleṉṉum oṉṟēyaṉṟi vēṟākāteṉak kūṟiviṭukka.
ākavē, pirāmaṇarkkuriya toḻil aintē yeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām. aracarkku aintum pirāmaṇarkku āṟumākat tol kāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatu. ulakiyaṟku māṟāṉa iṉmai naviṟciyē eṉa aṟika.
6. antaṇar vēṟu, pirāmaṇar vēṟu
antaṇar eṉpavar, irutiṇai yuyirkaḷiṭattum aruḷ kūrntu. tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu aimpulaṉaṭakki. iṉamoḻi kulamata iṭa vēṟupāṭiṉṟi ulakap potuvāy uḷḷan tūyarāya uyarnilai iṟaivaṉaṭiyār.
antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmai pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ  (kuṟaḷ 30)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. antaṇariṉ piṟaviyalpukaḷait tirukkuṟaṭ pāyira, nīttār perumai yatikārattiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
pirāmaṇarō, māṇava (piramacariya) nilaikkup piṉ il vāḻkkai (kirukattam) kāṭuṟaivu (vāṉappirattam) eṉṉum irunilaikaḷilum iyaṉṟavarai iṉpantuyttu, aimpoṟiyun tāmē aṭaṅkiya piṉ tuṟavuk kōlam (canniyācam) pūṇṭu. kulaveṟiyum moḻi veṟiyum nīṅkātu. āṇavamum tannalamum mikka āriya iṉattār.
itaṉāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟitta antaṇar vēṟu; parimēlaḻakar kuṟitta pirāmaṇar vēṟu; eṉa aṟintu koḷka.
7. nikaṇṭukaḷ āriyamuṟai taḻuviyaṉa
tamiḻ nikaṇṭukaḷellām tamiḻakattil āriyam vērūṉṟiyapiṉ tōṉṟi yavaiyātalāl, piṟaviyoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa kulavoḻukkam  paṟṟi avai kūṟupavaiyellām āriyac cārpāṉavēyākum. uriccol eṉṉum peyar māṟi nikaṇṭu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām.
iyaṟṟamiḻp poruḷilakkaṇam. kalvi kāval vaṇikam uḻavu eṉṉum nāṟperun toḻilpaṟṟi akapporuṭ ṭalaivarai nāl vakuppārāka vakuttatē, antaṇar. aracar. vaṇikar. vēḷāḷar eṉṉum tamiḻp pākupāṭām. illaṟattārāṉa tamiḻp pārppārum, akkālattil aruḷāḷarāy antaṇar eṉṉum peyarukkut takutiyuṭaiyārāyiruntaṉar.
ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tolkāppiyar, tam kālatti lēyē āriyam vērūṉṟiyataik kaṇṭataṉāl, tuṟaviyar pōl vīṭu vācaliṉṟi aṅkumiṅkum alaintu tirintu koṇṭirunta pirāmaṇaraiyum antaṇarākak koṇṭu.
nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇaiyē
āyuṅ kālai antaṇark kuriya  (tol. poruḷ mara. 71)
eṉṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, vaṇikarkku vāṇikamum vēḷāḷarkku uḻavumē taṉicciṟapput toḻil eṉṉum uṇmaiyai,
vaicikaṉ peṟumē vāṇika vāḻkkai  (tol. poruḷ mara. 79)
vēḷāṇ māntark kuḻutū ṇallatu
illeṉa moḻiya piṟavakai nikaḻcci   (tol. poruḷ mara. 82)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāṟ kuṟittār.
iṅṅaṉamiruntum, nikaṇṭukaḷ, antaṇar aracar mutaliya nāl vakuppāṉa tamiḻp pākupāṭṭai, āriyar (pirāmaṇar), pirāmaṇar cattiriyar vaiciyar cūttirar eṉat tannalattiṟkēṟpa muṟaiyē paṭimuṟait tāḻvuṟṟa nilaiyāṉa nāṟperum piṟavik kulap pākupāṭākat tirittu. pirāmaṇarkku āriya maṟaiyāṉa vētamōtu talum vēḷvi ceyvittalum, cattiriyarkkup pōr puritalum vēḷvi ceytalum, vaiciyarkku vāṇikamum uḻavum ceytalum, cūttirarkkuk kaittoḻilum mēl mūvakup pārkkum ēval ceytalum, taṉicciṟapput toḻileṉa vakutta muṟaiyaip perumpālum piṉpaṟṟiyē, āriyarallātavarum vētamum vēḷviyum vēṇṭātavarumāṉa tamiḻ nālvakup pārkkum avvāṟu toḻil vakuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷamai, tamiḻ marapiṟkum tamiḻar uyarvukkum eḷḷaḷavum ēṟkāta iḻi nilaiyum kaṭaippaṭṭa aṭimaittaṉamumē kāṭṭum.
8. āriyattai etirppatē tirukkuṟaḷ nōkkam
tiruvaḷḷuvar aṟamporuḷiṉpam eṉṉum muppālpaṟṟi iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya aṟanūl vaiyakam muḻuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭum vāyuṟai vāḻttē yāyiṉum, tamiḻarai āriya aṭimaittaṉat tiṉiṉṟu viṭuvippatē ataṉ aṭippaṭai nōkkameṉpatu.
antaṇa reṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkuñ
centaṇmai pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ. (30)
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkkai yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ.  (46)
oḻukka muṭaimai kuṭimai yiḻukkam
iḻinta piṟappāy viṭum.  (133)
maṟappiṉu mōttuk koḷavākum pārppāṉ
piṟappoḻukkaṅ kuṉṟak keṭum.  (144)
avicorin tāyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
uyircekut tuṇṇāmai naṉṟu.  (259)
īṉṟāḷ pacikāṇpā ṉāyiṉum ceyyaṟka
cāṉṟōr paḻikkum viṉai.  (666)
piṟappokku mellā vuyirkkuñ ciṟappovvā
ceytoḻil vēṟṟumai yāṉ.  (972)
cuḻaṉṟumērp piṉṉa tulaka mataṉāl
uḻantu muḻavē talai.  (1031)
uḻuvā rulakattārk kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟātu
eḻuvārai yellām poṟuttu.  (1035)
iravā rirappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
kaiceytūṇ mālai yavar.  (1033)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷkaḷālum, ivai pōṉṟa piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyap paṭum. iṅṅaṉam āriyattaik kuṟippākavum veḷippaṭai yākavuṅ kaṇṭippavarum.
tantai makaṟkāṟṟu naṉṟi yavaiyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal  (67)
eṉṟu kalviyai ellā makkaṭkum potuvākak kūṟiyavarum.
vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉaiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉai yavar  (410)
eṉṟu vētattaik kuṟiyātu vēṟu viḻumiya nūlkaḷaiyē kuṟitta varumāṉa tiruvaḷḷuvar. pirāmaṇarai orupōtum pōṟṟā reṉat  teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
iṅṅaṉam avar tamiḻarkku viḻippuṇartti īrāyiram āṇ ṭiṟku mēlākiyum, iṉṟum avar karuttaṟiyātu. parimēlaḻakar uraiyai maṟukkap pāril evaruḷḷār eṉṟu tarukkum tamiḻp pulavarum  uḷar. avar pētaik kuraittālun tōṉṟā tuṇarvu eṉṉum paḻamoḻik kūṟṟai (93) viḷakkap piṟantavarē yāvar.
iṉi, aṟutoḻilōr yāreṉiṉ, 
uḻavu toḻilē varaivu vāṇikam
viccai kaṟpam eṉṟit tiṟattaṟu
toḻil kaṟpa naṭaiyatu karumapūmi
eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpāviṟ kuṟitta aṟutoḻilaik kaṟṟavarē yeṉka.  - centamiḻc celvi - 1970
aṟaippaṭuttal
aṟaippaṭuttal eṉiṉum aṟaipōkkutal eṉiṉum kīḻaṟuttal eṉiṉum oṉṟē. kaiyūṭṭāl kāṭṭik koṭukkac ceytal eṉpatu ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷ.  (kuṟaḷ 747.)
aṉṉam
OE Ganra, OHG ganazzo, RV hamsa - aṉṉam, ekiṉam ōtimam eṉpaṉavē teṉ coṟkaḷ. mēlaiyāriyac coṟkaḷ muṟaiyē peruvāttaiyum ataṟkiṉamāṉa oru kaṭaṟpaṟavaiyaiyum kuṟippaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa hamsa eṉṉum kīḻaiyāriyac collē, ōtimattiṉ peyarāka vaḻaṅkivaruvatākat terikiṉṟatu. Swan eṉpatu āṅkilac col.  (ti.ma.piṉ.)
aṉal
aṉal - aṉala
aṉ - aṉṟu, aṉṟutal = ciṉattal. aṉṟu - kaṉṟu.
kaṉṟutal = ciṉattal, veyilāṟ karukutal.
aṉ - aṉal - kaṉal = neruppu, kaṉali = katiravaṉ.
aṉal = tī, veppam, iṭi. aṉalutal = aḻalutal.
aṉali = neruppu, katiravaṉ.
aḻal, aṉṟu. aṉal eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ mūlam al eṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum. atu vaḻakkaṟṟatu. atu el eṉṉum colloṭu toṭarpuḷḷatu. el = katiravaṉ, veyil, oḷi, pakal.
vaṭamoḻiyil aṉ eṉpatai mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. ataṟku uyirttal, iyaṅkutal eṉṉum poruḷē uṇṭu. - va.va.81.

ākamam
vētappirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantavuṭaṉ, matakkoṇ muṭipu, kōyilamaippu, kōyil vaḻipāṭu, mantira moḻikaḷ mutaliyaṉapaṟṟiya nūlkaḷai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. avai civa neṟi paṟṟiyavāyiṉ ākamam eṉṟum tirumālneṟi paṟṟiyavāyiṉ samhitai yeṉṟum, cāktampaṟṟiyavāyiṉ tantram eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. ākama = vantatu, tōṉṟiyatu, vaḻivanta nūl.
palvakai nūlkaḷ
pāṇiṉīyam (aṣṭātyāyī) iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.

antā, antō.
cēymaik karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟakkūṭiya piṟa karuttukkaḷ nīkkam maṟaivu mutaliya oru cilavē yātalālum, avaiyellām ūkārac cuṭṭuk karuttukkaḷuḷ ovvoru vakaiyil aṭaṅki viṭṭamaiyālum, ākārac cuṭṭiṉiṉṟu cēymai yoḻinta vēṟek karuttum paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyila.  (mu.tā.)
ākāraccuṭṭuk karuttu
ākārac cuṭṭu, oru ticaiyaiyuñ ciṟappāyc cuṭṭātu cēymaiyai maṭṭuṅ kuṟittalāṉum, orucāra cēymai kaṇmuṉ tōṉṟum aṇmaikkappāṟ paṭṭatātalāṉum, cēymaiyiṉ toṭarcciyellām cēymaiyēyātalāṉum, cēymaik karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟoru karuttun tōṉṟutaṟkiṉṟu. (mu.tā.)
ākāraccuṭṭu vērccoṟkaḷ
(1) cēymaiccuṭṭu (Remoteness) ā = anta (atu, avai).
āṅku - aṅku Cf. A.S. thar, E. there, Sans. tatra, āṅkar > (yāṅkar) > (nāṅkar) > ñāṅkar, āṇṭu, Cf. E. yon. yond, yonder, A.S. geond, Ger. jener, that, root ya.
ātu - atu, atā, atō, atōḷi, atu - ataṉ (te.).
am: antu, anta, antā, o.nō. um > untu.
	ammai = cēymaiyiṟ ceṉṟuviṭṭa muṟpiṟappu.
ā - av: (avaḷ), avaṇ, avaḷ = avviṭam.
	āṉ > avaṉ; ām > avam.
	avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, ava - avai.
al:	al + tu = aḵtu, o.nō. pal + tu = paḵtu.
	al + tu = aṉṟu. o.nō. nal + tu = naṉṟu.
lakaravīṟṟuccuṭṭu viṉāvaṭikaḷum uṇṭu eṉpatai ala ēlā eṉṉum teluṅkuc coṟkaḷālum alli illi elli eṉṉum karunaṭac coṟkaḷālum uṇarka. Cf. L. ille, he. Ar. al. the.
āṉ - aṉ : aṉṉa, aṉṉā (cuṭṭu); aṉṉa (such) - cuṭṭāc cuṭṭu. aṉṉa (uvamaiyurupu), aṉṉatu, aṟṟu.
aṉai (cuṭṭu), aṉai + tu = aṉaittu, aṉai, aṉaiya (uvamai.) 
akam = avvulakam, vīṭu (heaven, bliss), vīṭu pōṉṟa taṅkiṭam, vīṭṭai nukarum uḷḷam, uḷḷiṭam, iṭam.
aruḷillārk kavvulaka millai poruḷillārk
kivvulaka millāki yāṅku.  (kuṟaḷ. 247)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, avvulakam ivvulakam eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē cēymaiyum aṇmaiyum paṟṟi vīṭṭaiyum ñālattaiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka . ēcumīṭpar tam māṇavarai nōkkip pēriṉpa vīṭu uṅkaḷ uḷḷattilirukkiṟatu eṉṟār. pēriṉpa vulakiṟkum ivvulaka iruppiṭattiṟkum vīṭu eṉpatu potup peyarā yiruttalai nōkkuka.
uṭampiṉukkuḷḷē uṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē uttamaṉ kōyilkoṇṭāṉ
eṉṟār tirumūlar.
appāl > Gk. apo; L. ap. ab, abs, a; Sans. apa; Ger. ab; E. ab, abs, a, off, of, away.
appōtu eṉṉuñ collē appa eṉṟu tiritalāl, appāl eṉpatu appa eṉṟu tirivatu viyappaṉṟu.
(2) paṭarkkaip peyar : paṭarkkaic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ cēymaip poruḷkaḷaik kuṟippaṉavātaliṉ, cēymaiccuṭṭiṉiṉṟu paṭarkkaip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āṉ - tāṉ (orumai) = avaṉ, avaḷ, atu. ām - tām (paṉmai) = avar avai.
Cf. Sans. tat; A.S. thoet; E. that; Ger. das, dasz; Gk. te; (sl.) A.S.thas;
E. those (pl.)
tāṉ eṉṉum peyarē taṉ eṉṟu kuṟukip piṉpu āriya moḻikaḷil takaravīṟāyt tirintateṉka.
oppu nōkka : tirumāṉ > srīmat; nuṉi - nuti - tuti. ta - ca pōli. e-ṭu: mātam - mācam; pittaṉ - piccaṉ.
āṅkilattiluḷḷa than eṉṉum oppīṭṭiṭaiccol, taṉṉiṉ eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripāyirukkalām. taṉṉiṉ = ataṉiṉ.
kaipporuḷ taṉṉiṉ meypporuḷ kalvi eṉpatai nōkkuka.
A.S. thanne; Ger. dann; E.than; from stem of The.
akkarai eṉṉum col oru nīrnilaiyiṉ maṟupakkattai allatu etirp pakkattaik kāṭṭuvatu pōl, appāl appuṟam eṉṉum coṟkaḷum oru poruḷiṉ maṟu allatu piṉpuṟattaikkāṭṭi, ataṉpiṉ kālappiṉmai yuṇarttum iṭaic coṟkaḷāyiṉa.
akal = cēymaiyiṟ cel, nīṅku, viri.
o.nō. paṭar = cel, viri. akal - āl - ālam (maram).
(3) micaimaikkaruttu : cēymai oru vakaiyil micaimai pōlutalālum, mēṭṭiṉaṭiyil niṟkumpōtu cēymai micaimaiyāyiruttalālum, cēymaic cuṭṭil micaimaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.

Gk. ana, an, up; Goth, ana; E, on.
(4) celavukkaruttum piṟakaruttum: al - cēymaippaṭu, acai, cel, varuntu, curuṅku, taṅku, nīṅku, muṭivaṭai, viri: o.nō. ē - iya - iyaṅku = acai, cel. cellal = tuṉpam.
oruvar cellutavāvatu cēymaippaṭutal; celvatiṉāl uṭampiṟku acaivum ataṉāl varuttamum piṟakkum; cellac cella uṭampu curuṅkittōṉṟum; irāvāṉavuṭaṉ ōriṭattil taṅkanērum; cellutal ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu nīṅkutalākum; nīkkamē muṭivu; oru poruḷiṉ viḷimpu nīṅkuvatu virital.
allāṭu = alai, varuntu; allā = varuttam; allal = varuttam, tuṉpam; alattal = varuntal; alakkaḻi = varuttu; alam = tuṉpam; alakkaṇ = tuṉpam; alamvā = alamā; alamarutal = varuntutal; alavu = varuntu; al =- alu; aluttal = acaital, kaḷaittal; alu + al = aluval = varuntic ceyyum vēlai, vēlai; alu = aluṅku; aluṅkutal = acaital; alukku = acaippu, icaiyacaippu (kamakam). al = ala = alai, alaital = acaital, palaviṭañ cellal, varuntutal; alai = iṭam peyarntu koṇṭēyirukkum nīrttirai. alaikkaḻi = varuttu. alai - acai. al - āl. ālutal = āṭutal. ālatti - āḷatti = viḷakkai vaṭṭamiṭṭu āṭṭutal, icaiyai vaṭṭamiṭṭu āṭṭutalpōl vaḷarttal; āl - ālu - āṭu. āṭutal = acaital, viḷaiyāṭutal, kūttāṭutal. āṭuvatu āṭai. acaittal = acaittu uṭuttutal, pulittōlai araikkacaittu eṉṉun tēvārat toṭarai nōkkuka. al - alku. alkutal = curuṅkutal. alku - aḵku. aḵkutal = curuṅkutal, nuṭpamātal, kūrātal.
e-ṭu: aḵkiya i u aḵkiyakaṉṟa aṟiveṉṉām. L.acuo, to sharpen, from root ak. sharp, E. acute, L. acus, a needle, E.L. acumen, sharpness, quickness of perception. E. acme, Gk. akme - ake, a point.
alku - alkul = curuṅkiya iṭai, iṭaiyiṉ kīḻppakkam, alku - alaku, kūriya pala poruḷkaḷ alakuppeyar peṟṟaṉa. avai katti, paṟavai mūkku, ilai, neṟkatir, nel mutaliyaṉa. paṟavai mūkkiṟkotta kaṉṉamum alakeṉappaṭṭatu. alaku - alakku = ilai. alakku - ilakku - ilai; alam = kūriya kalappaikkoḻu, kalappai.
alku - aḵku - aḵkam - nel, kūlam (tāṉiyam). aḵku - akku akkam = kūlam, vitai, maṇi. uruttira + akkam = uruttirākkam = civamaṇi; al - alku; alkutal = taṅkutal; alkunar = taṅkupavar, vacippavar; al - alku = taṅkum irākkālam, irā; al + avaṉ = allavaṉ - alavaṉ = iravil tōṉṟum nilā, iravil veḷiyēṟum naṇṭu. iṉi kuṟaṭṭiṉ alakupōṟ kauvukiṉṟa kālaiyuṭaiyatu naṇṭu eṉṟumām. alavu - alaku. al - aṟu; aṟutal = nīṅkutal, oṭital; aṟuti = muṭivu. ar eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya aṟu (cut) eṉṉuñ colvēṟu.
al - alar - malar. cēymaippaṭuvatāl virital uṇṭākum. o.nō. akal = cel, viri; paṭar = cel, viri; oru poruḷ muḻutum ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu cellutal nīṅkutalām; ataṉ maiyam niṟka maṟṟap pakutikaḷ maṭṭum iṭam peyartal viritalām. malar - mallā, mallāttal = malarntu mēṉōkkippaṭuttal. mallā x kuppuṟu; kuppuṟu = kuvi, kīḻnōkkippaṭu. al - ala - alacu; alacutal = acaittal, acaittukkaḻuvutal, alaital, varuntal, nīṅkiyiruttal; alacaṭi = varuttam; alacu - alacal = iḻai nīṅkiyirukkum āṭai; alavu - alaval = alacal, kaṟpiṉmai (kaṭṭiṉmai); al - ala - alam - alampu. alamputal = acaittal, acaittuk kaḻuvutal. alampu - alappu: alapputal = vāyacaittal, pitaṟṟal. alaṭṭu = acai, varuttu, pitaṟṟu. alati, alakku - nīkkam, vēṟu. o.nō. viḷ - vil - vilu - viṟu - vīṟu - vēṟu = vilakiṉatu, vēṟāṉatu . alati - alāti. alakku - alākku. ittamiḻc coṟkaḷē intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāṉ alāti, avaṉai alākkāyt tūkkikkoṇṭu pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku. alavai  - pitaṟṟal, vāyāṭip peṇ, kaṟpiṉmai.  (cu.vi.)
āṅkilarāṭci
āṅkilarāṭci 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṭaiyil tōṉṟi, ivvirupa tām nūṟṟāṇṭiṭaiyil nīṅkiyatu. araciṉar aluvalakaṅkaḷi lum, peruñcālaikaḷilum, potuviṭaṅkaḷilum, pukai vaṇṭikaḷilum, miṉvaṇṭikaḷilum, uyarkalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷilum, vakuppu vēṟṟumai nīkkappaṭṭatu. āṅkilak kalvi ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiṟṟu. ellāt tuṟaiyilum, poyyum puraṭṭum kaṭṭuṅ kataiyumāṉa āriyat tolkatai (purāṇa) muṟaik kalviyiṉṟi, uṇmaiyum akakkaraṇa vāṟṟalai vaḷarppatum aṟiyāmaiyaiyum aṭimaittaṉattaiyum aṟavē akaṟṟuvatumāṉa, aṟiviyaṟkalvi pukaṭṭappaṭṭatu. kalvit tiṟamai mikka aṉaivarkkum vakuppu vēṟṟumaiyiṉṟip paṭipputavi (Scholar-ship) yaḷikkappaṭṭatu. intiyā muḻutum paṭippaṭiyāka oru pēraraiya āṭcikkuṭ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. āṅkilarāṭci yirunta, ilaṅkai kāḻakam (parmā) malaiyā teṉṉāppirikkā mutaliya pala veḷināṭukaḷilum, intiyar kuṭiyēṟit tamakkēṟṟa toḻilum aluvalum peṟṟu ēntāka vāḻntaṉar. āṅkilavaracu matattuṟaiyil talaiyiṭavēyillai. kula mata kaṭciyiṉa vēṟu pāṭiṉṟi, eḷiyārkkum valiyārkkum ēḻaikaṭkum celvarkkum, orē naṭunilai nayaṉmai (nīti) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, āṅkilarāṭci nērmaiyaiyum putuccēri kāraikkāl teru nērmaiyaiyum oruṅku nōkki, piriṭṭicu nītiyum pireñcu vītiyum. eṉṟu paḻamoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkumāṟu, potumakkaḷ pukaḻntu pārāṭṭiṉar. kumukāya iṉpa vāḻkkaikkum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum muṭṭukkaṭṭaiyāyirunta, uṭaṉkaṭṭai yēṟal, ceṭiṟkuttal (Hook-swinging), kuḻantai maṇam, narak kāvu (human sacrifice) mutaliya kuruṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum; takkar, piṇṭāriyar, tīvaṭṭik koḷḷaikkārar mutaliya koṭiya kayavar kūṭṭaṅkaḷum; aṟavē oḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. arumaiyāṉa añcal tuṟaiyum,  kuṟainta celavil viraintu vaḻiccellum iruppup pātaikaḷum , aḻakiya māṭa māḷikaikaḷum malainakar kaḷum, intiyāveṅkum amaintaṉa. uyirukkum poruṭkum cētamiṉṟi amaitiyāka vāḻumāṟu ciṟanta ūr kāvaloḻuṅkum, kalakamum pōruṅ kaṉaviluṅ kāṇāvāṟu māperum paṭai yamaippum, ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ellāt tiṇaikkaḷaṅkaḷilum (departments), intiyar tattam kalvikkum tiṟamaikkum takkavāṟu poṟuppu vāynta patavikaḷil amarttap peṟṟaṉar. intiyā muḻutum oṉṟupaṭṭu nāḷaṭaivil taṉṉāṭci peṟumāṟu, intiyat tēciyap pērāyam (Indian National Congress) kiyūm (hume) eṉṉum āṅkilap perumakaṉārāl 1885-il tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟatu. ataṉ payaṉāka, pērāyat talaivarkaḷ, āṅkilar nēraṭiyāṭci maṇṭalaṅkaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟi, ellā uḷnāṭṭu maṉṉar nāṭukaḷuḷḷum urimaiyoṭum pātukāppoṭum pukuntu, viṭutalaip pōrāṭṭattiṟku vitaikaḷai vāriyiṟaittu vantaṉar. iṟutiyil, 1947ām āṇṭu muḻu veṟṟiyum peṟṟaṉar. tantai makaṉiṭattiṟ cottaiyum, aracaṉ iḷavaracaṉiṭattil nāṭṭaiyum, takunta paruvattil oppaṭaippatupōl, āṅkilarum pērāyat talaivariṭam intiyāvai oppaṭaittuviṭṭu, amaitiyāy akaṉṟaṉar. ataṉāṟ pireñciyarum pōrttuk kīciyarum caṟṟup pinti intiyāvai viṭṭu nīṅka nērntatu.

āṭcimuṟai
aracavoḷi: paṇṭait tamiḻāṭciyil, aracaṉē nāṭṭiṟkut taṉi nāyakamāyiruntāṉ. perumpālum, avaṉ iṭṭatu caṭṭamum vaittatu varicaiyumāyiruntatu. muḻu niṟaivāṉa talaimaiyum uṭaṉ kollum atikāramum avaṉukkirunta tāl, avaṉ kuṭikaḷ yāvarālum kaṇ kaṇṭa teyvamākavē karutappaṭṭāṉ. ‘âUth¡»‰F vâ®th¡F©nlh?, aracaṉ aṉṟu kollum teyvam niṉṟu kollum eṉṉum paḻamoḻikaḷ aracaṉukkirunta atikāra valiyai yuṇarttum.
aracaṉuk kiṉṟiyamaiyātavarum avaṉuk kaṭutta paṭiyāy atikāra muṭaiyavarumāṉa amaiccar paṭait talaivarum, avaṉai mika aṇukātu tīkkāyvārpōl otuṅki yoḻukiṉar. avaṉ muṉṉilaiyil, piṟaroṭu pēcāmaiyum nakāmaiyum cāṭai kāṭṭāmaiyum, avaṉaic cuṭṭāmaiyum, avaṉ piṟaroṭu mellap pēcuvatai uṟṟuk kēḷāmaiyum, avaṉoṭu payilvār pōṟṟik kākkavēṇṭiya oḻukka neṟikaḷākum. aracaṉiṭattil oṉṟaik kūṟuvōr avaṉai vāḻttiyē kūṟuvatum. avaṉaik kāṇumpōtum avaṉaiviṭṭup piriyumpōtum perumakkaḷ talai vaṇaṅkiyum potumakkaḷ nilattil viḻuntum kumpiṭuvatum vaḻakkam.
aracavuṭaimaikaḷ teyvavuṭaimaikaḷ pōl tiruveṉṉum aṭaimoḻi peṟṟa peyarālum, aracaviṉaikaḷ teyva viṉaikaḷ pōl aruḷ eṉṉum tuṇaiviṉai koṇṭa viṉaiccollālum, kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
aracaṉ amaiccaroṭum paṭaittalaivaroṭum cūḻntu viṉai ceyvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatēṉum. avaṉ virumpiyatai niṟai vēṟṟaṟku etuvun taṭaiyāyiruntatillai. talaimai amaicca ṉukkum paṭait talaivaṉukkuṅkūṭa, aracaṉāl viṉavappaṭṭa kāriyattaippaṟṟi avar tam karuttait terivikkum urimaiyē yaṉṟi, avaṉaik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum atikāram iruntilatu. mēlum amaiccar paṭaittalaivaroṭu cūḻntē etuvuñ ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṉum yāppuṟavum aracaṉukkirunta tillai. āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, kōvalaṉaik kolvit tatum, putaiyalurimai eṭuttavarkkē eṉṟu paṟai cāṟṟuvit tatum, itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷām.
aracaṉ ceyal - aracaṉ taṉ tuṇaiyatikārikaḷum pulavaruṅ kūṭiya avaiyuṭaṉ ariyaṇaiyil vīṟṟiruttal, aracu vīṟ ṟirukkai eṉṟum makiḻirukkai eṉṟum olakkamirukkai eṉṟum kolu virukkai eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. atu perum pālum kālaiyil toṭaṅkumātalāl, atai nāḷavai nāṇ makiḻurukkai nāḷōlakkam eṉak kūṟuvatuṇṭu. aracaṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkum maṇṭapam, vēttiyaṉ maṇṭapam, ōlakkamaṇṭa pam, kolu maṇṭapam, attāṇi maṇṭapam, capāmaṇṭapam eṉṉum peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. am maṇṭapat tiṟkuc ciṟappup peyariṭuvatumuṇṭu. caṭāvarmaṉ kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ ōlakkamaṇṭapam pukaḻāparaṇam eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntatu.
aracaṉ ovvoru kāriyattaiyum ataṟkuriya ciṟappuk kuḻuvuṭaṉ allatu atikāriyuṭaṉ kalantu eṇṇuvāṉāyiṉum, aṉaittuk kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum talaimaiyamaiccaṉōṭu kūṭic cūḻnta piṉṉarē oru muṭiviṟku varuvaṉ.
aracirukkaiyilum cūḻviṉaiyilum araciyum uṭaṉiruppatu vaḻakkam.
aracaṉ ekkāriyattaic ceytālum, kaṇiyaṉ kuṟitta naṉṉāḷilum maṅkala vēḷaiyilumē ceyvaṉ.
ūrccapaiyār, nāṭṭatikārikaḷ, kōyiṟ kaṇkāṇippāḷar, potumakkaḷ mutaliyavaruḷ, taṉippaṭṭavarō oru kuḻuvārō oru kāriyattiṟku aracaṉuṭaiya oppattaiyēṉum tīrppai yēṉum peṟavēṇṭi avaṉiṭam variṉ, avar tāmē nērilāvatu uṭaṉ kūṭṭattatikārikaḷuḷ oruvar vāyilākavāvatu aracaṉukku atait terivippar. aracaṉukku mika vēṇṭiyavarāyiruntā loḻiya nēril terivikka muṭiyātu. aracaṉ akkāriyattai ataṟkuriyavaruṭaṉellām cūḻntu. taṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyai nēri lāvatu uṭaṉ kūṭṭattatikārikaḷuḷ oruvar vāyilāka vāvatu piṟappippaṉ.
aracaṉuṭaiya āṇaikaḷai ōlaiyi leḻutumpōtum piṉṉar avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟaik kallil veṭṭumpōtum, avai piṟanta iṭattaiyum amayattaiyum piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippatu vaḻakkam.
	kaṅkai koṇṭa cōḻapurattuk kōyiluḷḷāl, tiru mañcaṉa cālaiyi leḻuntaruḷiyiruntu utakam paṇṇi yaruḷā niṟka.
	srī rājarāja tēvar tañcāvūrp periya ceṇṭu vāyiṟ cittira kūṭattut teṟkil kallūriyil eḻuntaruḷi.
	kāñcīpurattuk kōyiluḷḷāl aṭṭattu veḷmēlai maṇṭapam rājēntira cōḻaṉil coṭṭaiyil eḻuntaruḷi yiruntu.
	puracai nāṭṭuc civapurattup pakalirukkaiyil tiruvamutu ceytaruḷā viruntu. (cōḻavamica carittirac curukkam, pak.49).
vēṟṟaracarum māṟṟaracarum viṭutta tūtar, vāyiṟ kāvalaṉ vāyilāy aracaṉukkut tam varavait terivittu, uttaravu peṟṟapiṉ ōlakka maṇṭapam pukuntu, tam aracar tirumukattai nīṭṭi, atu paṟṟiya viṉākkaṭkellām añcātu viṭai kūṟuvar. aracaṉ taṉ avaiyuṭaṉ naṉṟāka āyntu ataṟkut takka viṭai viṭuppaṉ. avvavvaracar tarattiṟkum naṭpaḷaviṟkum takkapaṭi, avar viṭutta tūtaraic cila pala nāṭkaḷ aracaṉ kākka vaippatu muṇṭu.
talainakarilirunta aṟaṅkūṟavaiyattil, potu vakaiyāṉa vaḻakkukkaḷaiyellām avaiyattārē tīrppar.
eṅkula vaṇika rēṟē yemmaṉōr vaḻakkai yintap 
puṅkava riṭaṉāt tīrttut tarukeṉap pulampi yārttār
naraimutu puliyaṉ ṉāṉcoṟ kēṭṭalum naṭuṅkic
cāṉṟōr, iruvar colvaḻakku mēṟkoṇ ṭaṉuvatit
tiraṇṭu nōkki
aṉaiyatu kēṭṭa vāṉṟō raṉaivarum nōkki yantat 
taṉapati vaṇikar tāmē yivareṉac cāṟṟa lōṭum
maṉavalit tāyat tārtam vaḻakkiḻuk kaṭainta tītu
naṉaivaḻi vēmpaṉ tēriṟ ṟaṇṭikku nammai peṉṉā 				(māmaṉākavantu vaḻakkuraitta paṭalam. 28,30,33.)
eṉṟu parañcōtimuṉivar, taṉapatic ceṭṭiyār vaḻakkai maturai yaṟaṅkūṟavaiyattārē tīrttatākak kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. ciṟappāṉatum aracaṉoṭu toṭar puṟṟatumāṉa vaḻakkāyiṉ. aracaṉē maṉṟattiruntu tīrppaṉ. cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟuttut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ. malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri makkaḷaip paṟṟiya ceytiyai. uṟantai maṉṟattiliruntu āyntamai kāṇka. īrūrārkkum, irukulattārkkum, iru matattārkkum, iru kōyilārkkum, īratikārikaṭkum, iru ciṟṟaracarkkum iṭaip paṭṭa vaḻakkāyiṉ; avvaḻakkāḷikaḷ talainakarkku vanta viṭattu aracaṉē tīrttuvaippatum, avar varātu aracaṉukkaṟi vitta (allatu avaṉiṭam muṟaiyiṭṭa) viṭattu atait tīrkkum paṭi avaṉ ōr atikāriyai aṉuppuvatum vaḻakkam. vaṉkolai yum muṟaikēṭāṉa kolait taṇṭamum nikaḻnta viṭattuk kolaiyuṇṭāriṉ āruyirk kēḷir aracaṉiṭam muṟaiyiṭu tal poruṭṭuc cōḻar araṇmaṉaip puṟavāyilil ārāycci maṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil aḵtillaiyā yiṉum, pāṇṭiyar kāṭcik keḷiyarāyiruntu attakaiya muṟai yīṭukaḷaik kēṭṭu muṟai vaḻaṅki vantaṉar eṉpatu, kaṇṇaki muṟaiyīṭṭā laṟiyappaṭum. iṉi, kaṭiṉamāṉa vaḻakkiṉ uṇmai kāṇṭaṟku iṟaivaṉ tirumuṉ pāṭukiṭappatum māṟukōlam pūṇṭu ceṉṟu potumakkaḷ kūṟṟaik kavaṉippatum, pāṇṭiyar vaḻakkam.
aracaṉ taṉ nāṭṭu nilaimaiyaip paṟṟi amaiccar vāyi lākavum, piṟar nāṭṭu nilaimaiyaip paṟṟit tūtar vāyilākavum, iraṇṭaiyum paṟṟi oṟṟar vāyilākavum, aṭikkaṭi aṟintu varuvaṉ. oṟṟaruḷ oruvaṉ kūṟṟai avaṉaṟiyātu iṉṉoruvaṉ kūṟṟoṭu oppunōkki, mūvar kūṟṟu ottaviṭattē atai aracaṉ nampuvāṉ.
taṉ nāṭṭu nilaimaiyaiyum taṉ nakara nilaimaiyaiyum tāṉē aṟivāṉvēṇṭi, iṭaiyiṭai nāṭukāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkkuc celvatum, māṟukōlam pūṇṭu nakara nōṭṭañ ceyvatum aracaṉ vaḻakkam. vikkiramac cōḻaṉ 1122 il kuṭantaikkaru kiluḷḷa paḻaiyāṟaiyilum, 1123 il ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭat taic cērnta kuḷivaḷanallūril oru kuḷakkarai maṇṭapattilum, 1124 il teṉṉārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattaic cērnta vīra nārāyaṇac caturvēti maṅkalam eṉṉum kāṭṭu maṉṉār kōyilil uḷḷa araṇmaṉaiyilum, 1130 il tillaik karukil ōr araṇmaṉaiyilum, iruntatākak kalveṭṭuk kūṟuvatāl, avaṉ aṭikkaṭi nāṭukāvaṟ cuṟṟup pōkkuc ceṉṟamai aṟiyappaṭum, maturaiyil koṟkaippāṇṭiyaṉ māṟukōlam pūṇṭu nakara nōṭṭam ceytu, kīrantai ūrilillātapōtu avaṉ vīṭṭiṟkuk kāvalāyiruntu vantatu nūṟpukaḻpeṟṟa ceytiyākum.
aracaṉ taṉ nāṭukāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkkil, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa atikārikaḷ tam kaṭamaikaḷaic cevvaṉē niṟaivēṟṟukiṉṟār kaḷāveṉak kavaṉippatum, ūrc capaiyārum aṟaṅkūṟavaiyat tārum tīrkkamuṭiyāta vaḻakkukkaḷait tīrttuvaippatum, kuṟṟañceytavarait taṇṭippatum, takkārkkut tāṉañcey vatum, kuṭikaḷ muṟaiyīṭukaḷaik kēṭṭu avarkaḷ kuṟaikaḷai nīkkuvatum, cīrtiruttamum makkaḷ nalamum paṟṟiya āṇaikaḷaip piṟappippatum, vaḻakkam.
iyaṟkaiyākavum ceyaṟkaiyākavum nāṭṭiṟkup peruntuṉpam nērnta kālaṅkaḷil, aracaṉ attuṉpaṅkaḷai iyaṉṟavarai nīkkuvatum, attuṉpa nīkkattiṟkākat toṇṭu ceytavarkku māṉiyam aḷippatum uṇṭu, veḷḷakkālattil karaikaṭṭi uṭaippaṭaippatum, aḻinta nilattai mīḷa aḷantu avvāṇṭu iṟainīkkik  koṭuppatum, pañcakālattil vari nīkkip paḻanel viṟpatum, puyaṟkālattil iṭinta kōyilkaḷaip piṉṉark kaṭṭuvatum, kalakaṅkaḷaip paṭaikoṇṭu aṭakkuvatum, koḷḷai nōyk kālattil (teyvakōpan taṇittaṟ poruṭṭup) paliyiṭṭu viḻaveṭuppatum, kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ nāṭṭil vantu cētañceyyiṉ avaṟṟai vēṭṭaiyāṭik kolvatum, kaṭalkōḷ nērntaviṭattup piṟanāṭṭup pakuti kaḷai veṉṟu avaṟṟil taṉ kuṭikaḷaik kuṭiyēṟṟuvatum, tuṉpa nīkkac ceyalkaḷām.
ciṟṟaracar ceyal: ciṟṟaraca nāṭukaṭkellām taṉṉāṭci (Self Government) aḷikkappaṭṭiruntatu. tiṟaiyaḷattal allatu paṭaiyutavip pōrāṟṟal ciṟṟaracar ceyalām.
aracaṉ valikuṉṟiyapōtu, aṉpaṟṟavarum atikāra viruppiṉaru māṉa ciṟṟaracar, aracaṉukkaṭaṅkātu taṉiyaracākiviṭuvatum avaṉuṭaiya pakaiyaracaroṭu toṭarpu koḷvatum uṇṭu. attakaiya camaiyaṅkaḷil aṉpum uṇmaiyum aṭakkamumuḷḷa ciṟṟaracar oṉṟu cērntu, aṭaṅkāc ciṟṟaracarkku māṟākat tamakkuḷ oppantañ ceytukoḷvatu muṇṭu. atu nilaimait tīṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
3 ām kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉiṉ 27 ām āṇṭil, kīḻkkuṟitta ciṟṟaracar patiṉmarum, aracaṉ cārpākavum avaṉukkaṭaṅkāta ciṟṟaracarkku māṟākavum tammuḷ oppantañ ceytu koṇṭaṉar.
(1)	pāṇṭināṭu koṇṭāṉāṉa campuvarāyaṉ.
(2)	ceṅkēṇi attimallaṉ vīrāṇṭāṉāṉa etirilic cōḻa campuvarāyaṉ.
(3)	kiḷiyūr malaiyamāṉ periyuṭaiyāṉāṉa irājarājac cētirāyaṉ.
(4)	kiḷiyūr malaiyamāṉ ākāracūraṉāṉa irāja kampīrac cētirāyaṉ.
(5)	kuntaṉ nampūralāṉa irājarāja nīlakaṅkaraiyaṉ.
(6)	ammaiyappaṉ marutaṉāṉa irājarāja mūvēntaraiyaṉ.
(7)	pāvantīrttāṉāṉa irājēntira cōḻac campuvarāyaṉ.
(8)	naraciṅka vaṉmaṉāṉa karikālcōḻa āṭaiyūr
nāṭāḻvāṉ.
(9)	cōmaṉ tiruvaṇṇāmalaiyuṭaiyāṉāṉa kulōttuṅka cōḻa pirutikaṅkaṉ.
(10)	cōmaṉ varantaruvāṉāṉa cōḷēntira ciṅkappiruti kaṅkaṉ. (mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ (irāmac cantira tīṭcitar) pak.87-88).
nāṭṭatikārikaḷ ceyal: nāṭṭatikārikaḷ tam nāṭṭaic cuṟṟippārttu, atiluḷḷa ūrccapaiyār kōyil kaṇkāṇip pāḷar aṟanilaiyap pātukāppāḷar mutaliyōr tattaṅ kaṭamaikaḷaic cevvaṉē niṟaivēṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉarāveṉak kavaṉippatum, ūrkkaṇakku kōyiṟkaṇakku aṟanilaiyak kaṇakku mutaliyavaṟṟait taṇikkaiyiṭuvatum, kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭa viṭattut taṇṭippatum, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa vaḻakkukaḷait tīrttu vaippatum, aracāṇaikaḷai niṟaivēṟṟuvatum, ūrccapait tērtalai naṭattivaippatum, ceyvar.
ciṟṟūrkaḷ nāṭṭatikārikaḷiṉ kaṇkāṇippilum pērūrāṉa nakarkaḷ aracaṉatu nēraṭiyāṉa kaṇkāṇippilum, iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ūrccapaiyār, tam kuṟṟattai maṟaittaṟporuṭṭum tammēṟ kuṟṟattai ēṟṟāmaipporuṭṭum, tammaik kaṇkāṇikkum nāṭṭatikārikaṭkum tammai viṉavavarum vēṟatikārikaṭkum kai yūṭṭum koṭuppatuṇṭeṉpatu; caṭāvarmaṉ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil, ōr ūrccapaiyār, vārappaṟṟiliruntum, kaṭamaip paṟṟiliruntum varum vārattaiyum kaṭamaiyaiyum, cuntarapāṇṭi yaṉuṭaiya atikārikaḷiṉ naṭpaip peṟac celaviṭṭati liruntu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. (Pandyan Kingdom p. 218-9).
ūrccapaiyār ceyal : ūrccapaiyāruḷ, ovvoru vāriyat tārum tattam kaṭamaiyai āṟṟivantaṉar. avaruḷ ūr vāriyat tiṉarkku, urimai (Civil) vaḻakku, kuṟṟa (Criminal) vaḻakku ākiya iruvakai vaḻakkaiyun tīrkkavum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷait taṇṭikkavum, muḻu atikāramiruntatu. avarāl tīrkkamuṭiyāta vaḻakkum, avar tīrtta vaḻakkiṉ mēṉ muṟaiyīṭum (Appeal) aracaṉiṭam koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa.
orē vāriyattiṟkuriya ceytiyai avvavvāriyattārum, pala vāriyaṅkaṭkuriya ceytiyai ellā vāriyattārum, kūṭic cūḻntu muṭivuceytaṉar. ūrccapaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kāḷamūtiyum paṟaicāṟṟiyum kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kūṭṭaṅ kūṭuvataṟku ūr maṇṭapam allatu māḷikai iruntatu. aḵtillā viṭattuk kōyil maṇṭapattil kūṭṭam naṭaipeṟṟatu.
avvappōtu varum aracāṇaikaḷait talaimēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟai niṟaivēṟṟivaippatum; avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉa vaṟṟaiyum ūrāṭciyil tām ceytuḷḷa putumaiyāṉa ēṟpāṭu kaḷaiyum, kallil veṭṭuvittuc capai maṇṭapattilāvatu kōyil maṇṭapattilāvatu patippippatum; ūrccapaiyār kaṭamaiyākum.
ūrk kuṭiyiruppaiyum, veḷḷāṇmaiyaiyum iraviṟ kāval ceyvatu pāṭikāvalaṉ vēlai.
ūravaiyāriṉ ūrāṭci, ūrāṇmaiyeṉṟum ūramai yeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
ciṟṟūrt talaivaṉ ceyal: āḷnilap pirivāṉa oru kūṭṭūriṉ pakutiyāy. orē kulattār allatu oru kulattār perumpāṉ maiyarāka irukkum ciṟṟūril, avvakkulattiṟkum iṭattiṟku mēṟpa, ampalakāraṉ, ūrāḷi, kavuṇṭaṉ, kiḻavaṉ, kuṭumpaṉ, cērvai (cērvaikāraṉ) talaimakaṉ, talaivaṉ, nāṭaṉ (nāṭāṉ, nāṭāḷvi), nāṭṭāṉ, nāṭṭampalam, nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, paṭṭaṅkaṭṭi, paṭṭattuppiḷḷai, paṇṇāṭi, periyataṉakkāraṉ, maṇiyakkāraṉ, maṉṟāṭi (maṇṇāṭi) mantiri. mūppaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟaip pūṇṭu. ūrārāl tērnteṭukkappaṭāmal tantaiyiṉiṉṟu makaṉukku varum vaḻimuṟai yurimaippaṭi ūrttalaivaṉāyiruppavaṉ, taṉ ūr vaḻakkukkaḷil kolaiyoḻinta piṟavaṟṟaiyellām tāṉē tīrttuvantatākat terikiṉṟatu. (Hindu Administrative Institutions in South India. p.262).
ciṟṟūriliruntu aracaṉukku varavēṇṭiya varikaḷellām oḻuṅkāy vantu, ūrār ūrttalaivaṉōṭu muṟṟum ottup pōyk kāriyaṅkaḷ amaitiyāy naṭantu vantatiṉāl, aracaṉum nāṭṭatikāriyum kūṭṭūrccapaiyārum ciṟṟūr vaḻakkut tīrppil perumpālum talaiyiṭavillai. kūṭṭūraic cērnta ellāvūr kaṭkum potuvāṉa ceytikaḷaiyum, ciṟṟūrc ceytikaḷil ūrttalaivaṉ atikārattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavaṟṟaiyumē, ūrc capaiyār kavaṉittu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
uttamaccōḻaṉ kālattil, toṇṭai nāṭṭaic cērnta ciṟṟiyāṟṟūril piramatēyamākak koṭukkappaṭṭa oru nilattaip paṟṟiya araca āṇai yōlaiyil, aṭikaḷ nakkaṉ eṉṉum palvattiri kiḻavaṉ (pavvattiri yūrttalaivaṉ) uṭaṉ kūṭṭattati kārikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu kaiyeḻuttiṭṭatālum, ūrttalaivaṉu ṭaiya ūḻiyattiṟkāka ampalamāṉiyam eṉṉum iṟaiyili nilam viṭappaṭṭiruntatiṉālum, ciṟṟūrt talaivaṉ patavi aracaṉāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭiruntatai aṟiyalām.
ciṟṟūrt talaivaṉukku ūrāṭciyil utavuvataṟku, cilavūrkaḷil, tuṇaittalaivaṉoruvaṉum karumamāṟṟuvōṉoruvaṉum cēvakaṉoruvaṉumāka mūvar iruntaṉar. ovvōr ūrkkum, nīrppāycci yoruvaṉum kuṭikkāval eṉṉum ūrkāvalaṉ oruvaṉum iruntaṉar.
ūrvaḻakkukkaḷait tīrppataṟkum ūril caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷai ēṟpaṭuttaṟkum ūrttalaivaṉ kūṭṭum kūṭṭam, ūrmaṇṭa pattilāvatu, oru marattaṭiyilāvatu, veḷḷiṭaiyilāvatu naṭaipeṟum.
āvaṇakkaḷariyār ceyal: āḷnila ūrtoṟum āvaṇak kaḷariyiruntatu. oru nilattai viṟpavaṉum koḷpavaṉum, allatu aṭaimāṉam vaippavaṉum vāṅkupavaṉum, allatu kuttakaikku viṭupavaṉum eṭuppavaṉum, tammuḷ icaintu eḻutiya āvaṇattai āvaṇakkaḷarikkeṭuttuk koṇṭu pōy, kaḷariyār muṉ tām iruvarum icaintu koṇṭatāka uṟutimoḻi kūṟiyapiṉ āvaṇam kaḷariyārāl kāppiṭap peṟum. kāppiṭutal eṉpatu aracu muttiraiyiṭṭup pativuceytal.
āvaṇattiṟku, ēṭu, ōlai, karaṇam, kalam, cīṭṭu, ceykai, tīṭṭu, naṟukku, paṭṭikai, paṭṭayam, muṟi mutaliya piṟa coṟkaḷum, avvav viṉaikkum eḻutappaṭṭa karuvikkum ēṟpa vaḻaṅkiṉa. āyiṉum, avaṟṟaiyellām taḻuvuvatum, atikāra eḻuttuc cāṉṟaic ciṟappākak kuṟippatum, āvaṇam eṉṉum collē pattiram piramāṇam eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻakku piṟkālatta tākum. ōlai cīṭṭu naṟukku muṟi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ paṉaiyōlaiyil eḻutap paṭṭavaṟṟaiyum, paṭṭikai paṭṭam eṉpaṉa ceppēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭavaṟṟaiyum, piṟa iraṇṭaiyum, kuṟikkum.
nilaviṟpaṉai yāvaṇattiṟku vilaiyāvaṇam, vilaiyōlai, vilait tīṭṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷum: āḷviṟpaṉai yāvaṇattiṟku āḷvilaittīṭṭu, āḷvilaippiramāṇa icaivuttīṭṭu, aṭimaiyōlai eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; vaḻaṅkiṉa. aṭaimāṉa āvaṇam, oṟṟikkalam oṟṟikkaraṇam oṟṟinaṟukku aṭaiyōlai eṉṟum; aṭaimāṉam vāṅkupavaṉ eḻutikkoṭutta ōlai, etiraṭaiyōlai yeṉṟum; peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. nukarcciyōṭu kūṭiya oṟṟikku iṟaṅkoṟṟi allatu aṭaippu (Usufructuary mortgage) eṉṟum nilaiyāṉa oṟṟikku uṟāvoṟṟi (Irredeemable mortgage) eṉṟum peyar. tīṭṭukkāraṉāl (Notary) eḻutappaṭāta allatu pativu ceyyappaṭāta oṟṟikkalam kaiyoṟṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. kuttakait tavaṇai muṭintavuṭaṉ ataṉ tīrvupaṟṟi eḻutappaṭuvataṟku aṟutimuṟi (Cancelled lease-deed) eṉṟum, oru poruḷiṉ urimai kuṟippiṭṭa oruvark kaṉṟip piṟarukkillaiyeṉṟu kuṟikkum eḻuttīṭṭiṟku viṭutīṭṭu allatu tīrvumuṟi (Release-deed) eṉṟum, peyar. oruvar oṉṟaṟ kuṭampaṭṭu eḻutittarum cīṭṭu, icaivut tīṭṭu icaiyōlai oppumuṟi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟatu. oruvar oṉṟaip peṟṟukkoṇṭatāka eḻutittarum, cīṭṭiṟkup paṟṟumuṟi (Receipt) eṉṟu peyar. nilavārac cākupaṭi yāvaṇam (Deed of lease for cultivation) kāṇip piṭipāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. 
vilaiyāvaṇam oḻinta piṟavaṟṟuḷ perumpālaṉa, iṉṟiyamai yātu āvaṇakkaḷariyiṟ pativuceyyappaṭa vēṇṭun tiṟattaṉavalla.
potuvakaiyāṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷ ōlaiyilum, ciṟappāṉavai ceppēṭṭilum, eḻutappeṟum. ūrārukkut teriyavēṇṭiya āvaṇamāyiṉ, kallil veṭṭappeṟum.
aṟaṅkūṟavaiyattār ceyal : nakaraṅkaḷilirunta aṟamaṉṟattār, vaḻakkāḷi (plaintiff) etir vaḻakkāḷi (Defendant) ākiya iruvariṉ collaiyum palamuṟai kēṭṭu, kariyum (witness) cāṉṟum (Evidence) iruppiṉ avaṟṟaik koṇṭum, avaiyillāviṭiṉ nūlutti paṭṭaṟivu (karutiyuktiyaṉupavam) koṇṭum, naṭunilai maiyāyt tīrttu muṟaiceytu vantaṉar.
iruvartam collaiyum eḻutaraṅ kēṭṭē
eṉṟu ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉum.
iruvar colvaḻakku nōkki yaṉuvatit tiraṇṭunōkki
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivarum, kūṟiyiruppatāl, vaḻakkai naṉṟāy aṟintukoḷḷaṟkum, vaḻakkāḷariṉ meymmai kāṇṭaṟkum, maṉṟattār vaḻakkaip palamuṟai colvittamai aṟiyappaṭum.
kariyuñ cāṉṟumillāta vaḻakkukkaḷil, paḻukkak kāycciya irumpaip piṭittal, pāmpuk kuṭattil kaiyiṭutal mutaliya teyvaccāṉṟu vāyilākat tam meymmaiyai nāṭṭumpaṭi, vaḻakkāḷarai maṉṟattār kēṭpatu muṇṭu. attakaiya cāṉṟu pala vaḻakkukaḷil uṇmaikku māṟāyiruntateṉpatu. pāḻntaṉicu vēṇṭip pāmpukkuṭam peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyālum.
kaiviṭṭa oṇporuḷ kaivara villeṉpār
meyppaṭṭa vāṟē uṇarntārāl - meyyā
maṭampaṭṭa māṉōkkiṉ māmayi laṉṉāy!
kaṭampeṟṟāṉ peṟṟāṉ kuṭam
eṉṉum paḻamoḻic ceyyuḷālum (57) aṟiyappaṭum. immuṟai piṟkālattuk kaiviṭappaṭṭatu.
vaḻakkukkaḷ, urimai vaḻakku (Civil case) kuṟṟa vaḻakku (Criminal case) eṉa ikkālattiṟ pirikkappaṭṭiruppatupōl ak kālattiṟ  pirikkappaṭavillai. iru vakai vaḻakkukkaḷum ellā aṟamaṉṟaṅ kaḷilum tīrkkappaṭṭaṉa.
paṭaittalaivaṉ ceyal : āṅkāṅku avvappōtu naṭakkuṅ kalakaṅkaḷaiyum koḷḷaikaḷaiyum, tāṉākavum taṉ tuṇai varaik koṇṭum aṭakkivaippatu, paṭaittalaivaṉ ceya lākum.  (pa.ta.ā.)

āttirēliya moḻikaṭkum tamiḻukkum uḷḷa oppumai
(1)	cakara vēṟupāṭukaḷum mūccolikaḷu millāmai.
(2)	piṉṉoṭṭuc coṟkaḷālēyē perumpālum putuccoṟkaḷ ākkappaṭal.
(3)	āttirēliyā moḻikaḷil uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷum aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷum vēṟupaṭuttappaṭāmai. mutupaḻan tamiḻilum iṅṅaṉamē yiruntatu.
	kā : maṇveṭṭi, viṟakuveṭṭi, calippāṉ (callaṭai).
(4)	ar paṉmaiyīṟā yiruttal.
tamiḻar, muṇṭar, nākar, āttirēliyar eṉpavar paṇṭu ōriṉat tārāyiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ilaṅkaiyil paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiyatu tamiḻeṉṟum, īḻanāṭṭara carkku muṭinākar eṉṟa pēr iruntateṉṟum, muttuttampip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ centamiḻc cuvaṭikaiyil (Magazine) eḻuti yiruppatu poruttamāṉatē.
