āytam
āyta eḻuttu vakaimai
eḻutteṉap paṭupa
akara mutala ṉakara viṟuvāy
muppaḵ teṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
avaitām
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa  (tol. nūṉmarapu 1, 2)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷāl mutaleḻuttu muppa teṉpatum, cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉpatum, ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉai yavē ciṟantaṉaveṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṟ cārntu varaṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppaḵteṉpa eṉavē, cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟumē ciṟantaṉa; ēṉaiya muppatum avvāṟu ciṟantila veṉavum poruṭantu niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉai vilakkic ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu ivaiyum oppa vaḻaṅku meṉṟaṟku, eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉṟār eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ip peyarkaḷē peyar, im muṟaiyē muṟai; tokaiyum mūṉṟē eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tēṟṟēkāramiṭṭuk kūṟu vatālum, naṉṉūlār cārpeṉak kūṟiya pattu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ ēṉaiya vēḻum tolkāppiyar kālattilum iruntamaiyālum, tolkāppiyarkkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum naṉṉūlār koḷkai yiruntatillai yeṉpatu teḷivām.
āytap peyarum oliyum
tolkāppiyar āytattaik kuṟikkumpōtē muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉa ataṟku maṟṟumoru peyar koṭuttār. piṟkālat tāciriyar vēṟuñ cila peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. āytam eṉpatu aḵkēṉam, aḵkēṉameṉiṉum āyta meṉiṉum taṉinilai yeṉiṉum puḷḷi yeṉiṉum oṟṟeṉiṉum okkum, eṉṉai?
aḵkēṉa māytan taṉinilai puḷḷi
yoṟṟip pāla vaintu mitaṟkē
eṉṟār mayēccurar eṉṟu kārikaiyuraiyāciriyarāṉa kuṇacā kararum, āytam eṉiṉum, akkēṉam eṉiṉum, taṉinilai eṉiṉum, puḷḷi eṉiṉum, oṟṟu eṉiṉum okkum, eṉṉai?
akkēṉa māytan taṉinilai puḷḷi
yoṟṟip pāla vaintu mitaṟkē
eṉṟār avinayaṉār eṉṟu yāpparuṅkala viruttikārarum kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
avinaya nūṟpāvil akkēṉameṉpatu ēṭṭuppiḻaiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum. paḻaiya tiṇṇaippaḷḷip pāṭamuṟaiyil āytappeyar  ataṉ oli vaṭivoṭṭi aḵkaṉṉā eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
āytavoli
ēṉaiyiru cārpeḻuttup pōṉṟē. āytamum oru mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripeṉpatu. itaṟku (āytattiṟku) vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. ēṉai yoṟṟukaḷ pōla uyirēṟātu ōcai vikāramāy niṟpatoṉ ṟākaliṉ, eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraiyālum,
cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
tērntuveḷip paṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum
tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
yotta kāṭciyiṟ ṟammiyal piyalum  (piṟappiyal, 19)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvālum, cārpeḻuttu eṉṉum peyarālum, aṟiyappaṭum. āyiṉ, evveḻuttiṉ tiripeṉiṉ, kakarattiṉ tiripām eṉka.
āytam toṉṟutoṭṭu melliya kakaramākavē olikkappaṭṭu varuvatālum, ēṉac cāriyai peṟṟakkāl aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattōṭiyaintē vaḻaṅku vatālum, paṇṭaiyēṭṭariccuvaṭi kaḷil ka - ḵ eṉṟu eḻutap peṟṟamaiyālum uyirukkum meykkum iṭaiyil vaikkappaṭṭiruppiṉum kakarattai yaṭuttirup patālum, pōppaiyar tam tamiḻilakkaṇattil ‘the guttural’ eṉṉum, kālṭuvel tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil “guttural h” eṉṟum kuṟittiruppatālum, ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyil ataṉ amaippu muṟaiyai viḷakkumiṭattu “the aspirate aytam” eṉṟu kuṟittiruppiṉum, varipeyarppil (transliteration) k eṉṉum kuṟiyai āḷvatālum āytaveḻuttu kakarattiṉ tiripēyeṉat teṟṟeṉa vaṟika.
āriya vaṉmai cāṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻukkiṉmaiyiṉ, āytattai vaṭamoḻi hakaram (h) pōṉṟa mūccoliyākak koḷvatu muṟṟum tavaṟām. iṉi vaṭamoḻi vicarkkam: pōṉṟa oliyeṉak koḷḷiṉ, oliyaḷavil vicarkkam āytattaiyot tiruppiṉum, atil akarak kūṟum kalantiruppatāl, atuvum poruntāteṉka. āytam evvakaiyiṉum uyirēṟappeṟā eḻuttām kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi eṉṉum nūṟpā vuraiyil, āytamāṉa puḷḷi, āytappuḷḷi eṉṟār. itaṉaiyum oṟṟiṉpāṟ cārttutaṟ keṉka. oṟṟēl uyirēṟap peṟal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ. cārpeḻuttātaliṉ uyirēṟap peṟāteṉak koḷka eṉa mayilainātar uraittiruppataik kāṇka.
āytam aṟṟālaḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uyirpōla alaku peṟṟum, tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka vaḵtilār mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil meypōla alaku peṟāmalum uyirmeykaḷai (oru puṭaiyottu) uyirumākāmal meyyumā kāmal (alipōlat) taṉiniṟṟalāl, taṉinilai eṉappaṭṭatu eṉṟār naṉṉūṟ kāṇṭikai yuraikārarākiya caṭakōpa rāmāṉucāc cāriyār. aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku āytam alaku peṟal vēṇṭāmai yāṉum, avvāccāriyārē uyirum meyyum tammiṟ kalattal pōla āytam ōreḻuttōṭuṅ kalattaliṉṟit taṉi niṟṟalāl taṉinilai yeṉappaṭṭa teṉṟalu moṉṟu. eṉak kūṟiyirutta lāṉum avvurai poruntāteṉka. āytam kakarattiṉpāṟ paṭṭa oru nuṇṇiya voli yāyiruttal paṟṟiyē appeyar peṟṟateṉ ṟaṟika.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam  (uriyiyal. 32)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. āytap peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟumiṭattu, “that which is subtle, minute” eṉṟār kālṭuvelum.
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻakarāti, tāṉ koṇṭa kuṟikkōṭkoppa, tamiḻc coṟkaḷait tamiḻvaḻi ārāyātu, āytam eṉṉum teṉcollaic cārntirukkaiyeṉṟu poruḷpaṭum āciritam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ ṟiripā yirukkalā meṉak karuti mayaṅkukiṉṟatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalikaram āytam eṉac cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟāyirukka, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṟku maṭṭum cārntirukkaip poruṭcol eṅṅaṉam peyarā yamaintiruttal kūṭum? vaṭamoḻiyait tamiḻukkaṭippaṭaiyāy vaittu, olimuṟaic colliyal (Sound Etymology) paṟṟit teṉ coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟai vaṭacolleṉak kūṟuvatu, vaṭamoḻi yāḷarkkut toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, āytam eṉṉum teṉcollai āyutam eṉṉum vaṭa colloṭu mayakki, aṭuppukkūṭṭum kēṭakam mūvilaic cūlam ākiya paṭaikkalamum pōl varivaṭivu koṇṭiruppatāl āytam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉak kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. āytam eṉṉum col oriṭattum āyutam eṉa eḻutap paṭāmaiyaiyum, āyutam eṉṉum collum āytam eṉa eḻutappaṭāmai yaiyum, nōkkuka. āriyar nāvalan tēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē, āyta veḻuttum ataṉ peyarum kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉṉum uṇmaiyaiyum aṟintu koḷka.
iṉi, yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuraikārar uyirē meyyē eṉṉum nūṟpāvuraiyil,
cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟum taṉmaiya eṉṟu mikuttuc colliya vataṉāl, akarattoṭu yakaravoṟṟu vantum āytam vantum aikārattiṉ payattavāy niṟkum ...
āytamum yavvu mavvoṭu variṉē
aiyeṉe ḻuttoṭu meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉṟār avinayaṉār.
varalāṟu ... kaḵcu, kaḵtam, kaḵcam eṉa akarattoṭu āytam vantu kaicu, kaitam, kaicam eṉa aikārattiṉ payattavāyiṉavāṟu ... eṉa uraittiruppatu. āytattaip poṟuttamaṭṭil uttikkup poruntuvataṉṟu. kaḵcu mutaliya mūṉṟum kaicu mutaliya mūṉ ṟākat tirintirukkalāmēyaṉṟi, āytam yakaramey yot tolit tiruttal muṭiyātu. orukāl, kaḷ eṉpatu kaḵcu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ aṭiyākaliṉ, atu kay eṉat tirintirukkalām. oppu nōkka : toḷ - toy, poḷ - poy.
āytap piṟappu
cārntuvari ṉallatu eṉṉum cārpeḻuttup piṟappu nūṟpāliṉ āṟṟoḻuk kaik koṇṭukūṭṭākac citaittu, iru kūṟākki, cila eḻuttukaḷaiccārntu tōṉṟiṉallatu tamakkeṉat tōṉṟutaṟku ōriya pilaveṉṟu, ārāyntu veḷippaṭukkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ tammuṭaiya piṟappiyalpu mūṉṟiṉaiyuṅ kūṟuṅkāl, tattamakku uriya cārpākiya meykaḷatu ciṟappup piṟappiṭattē piṟattalōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟum, oḻinta āytan tamakkup poruntiṉa neñcu vaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum eṉṟum uraittaṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
in nūṟpā muḻuvataiyum orē kūṟṟākak koṇṭu, cila eḻuttukaḷaic cārntuvariṉallatu tamakkeṉat taṉiyiyal pillātaṉaveṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa (kuṟṟiyalu karamum kuṟṟiyalikaramum āytamumākiya) maṟṟa mūṉṟu eḻuttu kaḷum, tattam mutaleḻuttukaḷiṉ piṟappoṭu porunti avaṟṟō ṭorupuṭai yotta tōṟṟattiṉavāyt tattam ciṟapput taṉmaiyuṭaṉ naṭakkum eṉṟuraippatē poruttamām.
iṉi āytan talaivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakku meṉpārum uḷar eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
āytac cāriyai
kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyai  (puṇariyal, 32)
eṉṉum eḻuttuccāriyai nūṟpāvil āytaveḻuttiṟkuriya cāriyaiyai āciriyar kūṟāviṭiṉum, nērattōṉṟum eṉavē nērat tōṉṟātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avai āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam eṉka. ivai citainta vaḻakkēṉuṅ kaṭiyalākāvāyiṉa eṉṟu uraiyāciriyar (nacciṉārkkiṉiyar) kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. tolkāppiya nūlil kūṟappaṭāta cila ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ urai yāciriyarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ āytaveḻuttuc cāriyaiyum oṉṟu. itu uraiyiṟ kōṭal eṉṉum utti paṟṟiyatu.
āytac cārpu
āytam, kuṟilukku muṉṉum valliṉa vuyirmeykkup piṉṉumāka avviraṇṭiṟkumiṭaiyil varuvatē iyalpām.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē  (moḻi marapu. 5)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
āytan tāṉē kuṟiyataṉ kīḻttāy
valiyataṉ mēlvan tiyalu meṉpa
eṉṟār kaiyaṉār.
iv vitikku, eḵku kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, aḵkam, veḵkā, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyurai yāciriyarum, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉak kuṇa cākararum, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉa mayilai nātarum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉar. ivai taṉic coṟkaḷ. aḵṟiṇai, muḵṭītu, aḵkakūṭiya eṉpaṉa toṭarccoṟkaḷ.
vilaḵḵki līṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē māta
rivaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam
eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟiliṇaik kīḻum āytam varumālōveṉiṉ, aṉṉa iyalpākavē āytamāy niṟpaṉavalla. ōcai niṟaittaṟ poruṭṭu oṟṟiṉ vaḻi oṟṟākkutaliṉ, īṇṭu āytamum oṟṟāy varumeṉak koḷka eṉṟu mayilainātar kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
laḷaḵkāṉ (nūṉmarapu. 24) ṇaṉaḵkāṉ (nūṉmarapu. 26) eṉat tol kāppiyattil vantiruppavai ummait toṭarkaḷāta liṉ, kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ vantaṉavākā. āyiṉ, iḷaḵkumē eṉac cintāmaṇiyil (149) vantiruppatu vitikku māṟātaliṉ, itaṉai yum oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟeṉavē koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
āytam tōṉṟum vakai
lakarattiripu. vakarattiripu, ḷakarattiripu, oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟu, cāriyaip puṇarppu eṉa āytam tōṉṟum vakai aintām.
takaram varuvaḻi yāyta nilaiyalum
pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulamai yōrē  (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 74)
eṉpatu lakarattiripu. kaḵṟītu, kaṟṟītu eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ivai uṟaḻcci, aḵṟiṇai paḵṟuḷi eṉpaṉa uṟaḻcciyillātaṉa.
vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yāyta mākum  (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 84)
eṉpatu vakarattiripu. aḵkaṭiya, iḵkaṭiya, uḵkaṭiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
āyta nilaiyalum varainilai yiṉṟē
takaram varūuṅ kālai yāṉa  (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 104)
eṉpatu ḷakarattiripu. muḵṭītu. muṭṭītu eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ivai uṟaḻcci.
oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟu muṉṉark kāṭṭappeṟṟatu.
aḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ, vaḵkāṉ eṉpaṉa cāriyaip puṇarppu.
aḵtu eṉpataṉ kuṟukkamē atu eṉat teritaliṉ. ceyvaḵtu eṉpatu ceyvatu eṉpataṉ virittal tiripeṉak koḷvatu cariyaṉṟu.
vakarattiripum cāriyaip puṇarppum ottiyalamaipu (assimilation) eṉṉum neṟimuṟai paṟṟiyaṉa.
āytam varum taṉic coṟkaḷ
āytac cārpaiyum āytan tōṉṟum vakaiyaiyum tuṇaik koṇṭu āytam varum taṉic coṟkaḷai āyntu pārppiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ perumpālāṉa lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē pōtarum. it tiripu kumarināṭṭiṉ oru maruṅku tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
e.kā. aḵku
alkutal = curuṅkutal, kuṉṟutal, nuṇukutal.
alku - aḵku.
aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu, eḵtu.
ivai, al + tu, il + tu, ul + tu, el + tu eṉṉum puṇarcciyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟu iṉṟu eṉṟu eṉṉum kālac cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷum, al il el eṉpaṉavē. ivaṟṟai, alli illi elli eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭa iṭaccuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷoṭum, aṉṟu (al + tu) iṉṟu (il+tu) naṉṟu (nal+tu) eṉṉum oṉṟaṉpāṟ kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷoṭum, oppunōkkuka.
al+tu = aḵtu - attu - atu. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaiyavum.
attoṭu niṉṟatu alaiccal, koṭṭoṭu niṉṟatu kulaiccal, ettāl vāḻalām ottāl vāḻalām eṉpaṉa paḻamoḻikaḷ.
paḵtu pal + tu = paḵtu - pattu - patu.
pal - paṉ = pattu. o.nō. āl - āṉ. vel - veṉ.
paṉ + iraṇṭu = paṉṉiraṇṭu.
paṉ - pāṉ = pattu. irupāṉ muppāṉ mutaliya eṇṇup peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya oṉṟu mutaṟ pattu varaippaṭṭa eṇṇup peyarkaḷuḷ, oṉpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa eṇṇaip pala eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟip pal eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu amaittatākat terikiṉṟatu.
eḵku
iḷ (- iḷa - iḷaku) - eḷ - eḷku - eḵku = urukku. atāvatu iḷakiṉa tātu (ulōkam). eḵku - eḵkam = urukkiṉāṟ ceyta vēl.
paḵṟi
pal + ti = paḵṟi (paṉṟi) = paṉṟi vaṭivāṉa oruvakaip paṭaku. itil carakkēṟṟic celvatai iṉṟum kūvam mutaliya āṟukaḷil pārkkalām.
veḵku
piḷ - piṇ - piṇā - piṇavu - piṇaval. piṇā - piṇai.
piḷ - peḷ - peṇ - pēṇ - pēṭu - pēṭai
peḷ - peṭṭai - peṭai.
peḷ - peṭpu = viruppam.
peḷ - veḷ - vey - veymmai - vemmai = vēṇṭal.
veḷ - vēḷ - vēṇ - vēṇṭu.
veḷ - veḷku - veḵku.
veḵkutal = piṟaṉ poruḷai virumputal.
kaḵṟu
kal - kāl = karumai.
kāltōy mēṉik kaṇṭakar (kamparā. vāṉara. 21)
kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḷam = karumai.
kaḷ + tu = kaḵṟu (karumai)
kaḵḵṟeṉṉuṅ kallatarattam  (tol. eḻut. 40. urai)
kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu mutaliya piṟa āytac coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷkaḷ maṟaintu pōyiṟṟeṉka.
āyta varivaṭivu
āytavoli, aṭuppil neruppai aṇaikkumpōtu uṇṭākum ōcaiyai orupuṭai ottiruppatāl, orukāl, ataṉ varivaṭivu aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl muppuḷḷi vaṭivāy amaik kappaṭṭirukkalām.
muṭipu
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl. āytam tamiḻeḻutteṉṟum. atu lakara ḷakara vakarat tiripāṉa nuṇ kakara meyyeṉṟum, oṉṟā yiruttalum uyirēṟāmaiyumpaṟṟit taṉinilai yeṉappaṭṭa teṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka. iṉi āytaveḻuttait tamiḻeḻuttu kaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum iṭṭu. ja. ṣa. F,Z mutaliya piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷai ikkālattuc cilar kuṟippatu. tamiḻiyalpiṟku māṟāṉatum tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷaikku muraṇāṉatumāku meṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyellām, avvāṟākkiṉ tamiḻ nāḷaṭaivil vēṟu moḻiyāka māṟiviṭumeṉṟum, aṟintu kaṭaippiṭikka. mēlum, āytavolikku ayaṉ moḻi yolikaḷaik kuṟikkum āṟṟalillaiyeṉṟum, āyta varivaṭi veḻutuvatum ayaṉ moḻi varivaṭiveḻutuvatum oṉṟē yeṉṟum eḻutteṉpatu uṇmaiyil oliyēyaṉṟi varivaṭivaṉ ṟeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.  (kaḻakattiṉ 1008āvatu veḷiyīṭṭu viḻā malar.)
āyam
āyam - āya (i.vē.)
tāyam - āyam = urimai, kaṭamai, kaṭattakkūli, tāya āṭṭakkāy, tāya eṇ allatu paṇaiyam.
uruḷāyam ōvātu kūṟiṟ poruḷāyam
pōoyp puṟamē paṭum.  (kuṟaḷ. 933).
āyattiṟ kañci āṟṟil nīntiṉatupōl.
eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
irukku vētattil āya eṉṉum col vantucērkai eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu (ii, 38: 20). pāṇiṉīyam, maṉumiruti, makāpāratam mutaliya piṉṉūlkaḷ varuvāy, vari, ūtiyam mutaliya poruḷkaḷil āḷkiṉṟaṉa. āya eṉṉum coṟku ā-i eṉṉum mūlaṅ kāṭṭuvar. i eṉpatu iy eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ citaivām. iy-iyal, iyaṅku.  (va.va. : 84.)
āyam : (ka) tāy - tāyam = tāyvaḻiyurimai. mutaṟkālattiṟ kuṭumpat talaimai tāṅkiyatu tāyē. tāyam - āyam = urimai. tāyam - tāya (va.).
āyam : (u) vā - ā - āyam = varuvāy, vari, kaṭṭaṇam. ōṭivā eṉpatu koccai vaḻakkil ōṭiyā eṉṟu collappaṭutal kāṇka. vā eṉṉuñ collē āv eṉṟu muṉpiṉṉāka muṟai māṟi intiyil vaḻaṅkum. āyam - āya (va.).  (ti.ma. 737.)
āyirattāḻi
kōlap pāṉaiyai āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉaiyeṉṟum, aracāṇip pāṉaiyeṉṟum āyirattāḻiyeṉṟuñ colva tuṇṭu.  (ta.ti.22.)
āyiram
āyiram - sakara (i.vē.)
ayir = nuṇmaṇal. ayir - ayiram - āyiram.
āṟṟu maṇalum kaṭaṟkarai maṇalum ērāḷamāyiruppatāl, maṇaṟ peyar oru peruntokaip peyarāyiṟṟu.
vāḻiya ... naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē.  (puṟam 9).
nī nīṭuvāḻiya ... vaṭuvāḻ ekkar maṇaliṉum palavē  (puṟam. 55).
ma. āyiram, ku. āyire, ka. cāvira, tu. cāvira, tuṭa. cōver (f), kō. cāvrm.
i. hacār (haza#r), pe. hacār (haza#r), ki khilloi for khesloi.
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamillai. akaramutal cakara mutalāyt tirivatum yakaram vakaramāy māṟuvatum iyalpātalāl, kaṉṉaṭattil āyiram eṉpatu cāvira eṉat tirintatu.
o.nō. iḷai - ciḷai, utai - cutai, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, ēṇ cēṇ. nīyir - nīvir, cēyaṭi - cēvaṭi.
kaṉṉaṭac collai yoṭṭiyē tuḷuvac collum, kaṉṉaṭat tiripāṉa paṭakac collai yoṭṭiyē tuṭavac collum kōttac collum, ivaṟṟai yoṭṭiyē vaṭacollum, cakara mutalāyt tirintuḷḷaṉa. itai aṟiyātu, pērā. parō vaṭa collaiyē teṉcoṟku mūlamākat tam akaramutaliyiṟ kāṭṭiyiruppatu, tamiḻaip paṟṟiya tam tavaṟṟuk karuttiṉālēyē.  (va.va. 84-85.)
ār
ār - ara (i.vē.) 
ārtal = poruntutal. ār = cakkarak kuṟaṭṭilum vaṭṭai yilum poruntum caṭṭam (spoke, radius).
ārkeḻu kuṟaṭucūṭṭāḻi pōṉṟu  (cīvaka. 828).
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṭṭappeṟum vēr ru (ri) eṉpatē. aṟu iri eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripākum.  (va.va.85.)
ārppāṭṭam
ārppāṭṭam - ārapāṭṭa (bh).
ārttal = āravārittal. ārppu + āṭṭam - ārppāṭṭam = āravāram. te. ārpāṭṭamu (bh).  (va.va.85.)
āram
āram1 - āra
ār - āram. ārañ cūḻnta ayilvāy nēmi (ciṟupāṇ. 253). va.va.85.
āram2 - hāra
ārtal = poruntutal. ār - āram = pala malarkaḷ allatu maṇikaḷ poruntiya mālai.
o.nō. toṭu - toṭai.  (va.va. 84.)
